# Data moat and coverage plan (mentor + Data Chief), 2026-10-18

Status: adopted by the mentor under the founder's delegation ("decide yourself, discuss with the team, go ahead"). Detail, day-by-day schedule and release-policy worksheet: `chief-plan-detail-2026-10-18.md`. Evidence trail: `log.md`. Everything stays reversible; nothing here changes the database by itself.

## Approach (changed after the Chief's challenge)
Work by **rule family**, chosen by **weighted reach to what users see**, verified by **sample**, checked by **code over the whole population**. Not company-by-company (only for the golden verification set and long-tail exceptions), not "map all tags" (12,013 unmapped; 3,534 used by five or fewer companies).

## What the Chief corrected in my first proposal (accepted)
1. **Correctness before coverage.** Known wrong-value classes go first: stored value differs from an authoritative copy (39 of 52,464 sampled rows), mislabeled period columns and full-year values under quarter labels, stale negative weighted-average share rows.
2. **"A cycle must move a number" was too crude.** New concepts grow the denominator. Use a fixed denominator for gains and a guard number that must not worsen; a held mapping that prevents a wrong value counts as a success.
3. **Denominator work must not look like a gain.** Report three states: value / proven-absent-from-face / unknown, with both denominators shown. `line_not_presented_in_filing` is evidence, not proof (14-34% of values we hold show no face line).
4. **Selection by rule family, weighted by company band** (top 100 / 1,000), not raw tag counts. A 12-case sample with 0 failures bounds the failure rate at only about 25%, so every family also gets a full-population code check.
5. **The apply step needs an approver.** See release tiers below.

## New finding that reorders the work (mine, measured 2026-10-18)
`analytics.metric_definition_input` has 88 inputs over 33 canonical concepts. The other 45 concepts, and all 10 face lines we just proposed, feed **no metric**. So the 10 proposals add data points but cannot raise the 74.61% metric coverage. To raise what users see, work first on the gaps inside the 33 consumed concepts and the metrics' own gap reasons (top reasons in the 2026-10-13 matrix: incomplete numerator 23,790; line not presented 13,903; incomplete add 10,645; incomplete subtract 9,332; incomplete denominator 8,121; missing ebitda_ttm 7,817; missing share_buybacks_ttm 5,652). The 10 face lines move to a second wave, or wait until a metric needs them.

## Order
1. **Wrong values out** (days 3-15): "authoritative same-tag copy wins" with precision tie-break and label-error detectors; weighted-average reprocess; verify by re-running the 150-company seed sample (target: 39 -> 0 except documented different-basis rows) and a fresh Verifier sample with 0 critical.
2. **Honest baseline and ledger** (days 1-5, parallel): fresh snapshot (the 2026-10-13 one is stale; diagnose why it stopped), three-state ledger, metric-to-concept inventory per metric.
3. **Metric-driven gaps in the 33 consumed concepts**: start with the biggest reasons above (EBITDA inputs, buyback inputs, incomplete numerator/add/subtract); each is a rule family with baseline -> propose -> define -> verify sample -> full-population check -> committed migration + reprocess -> re-measure.
4. **Quarter derivation** after step 1's upstream fix: additive allowlist only (cash-flow items, tax, pretax); EPS and shares by their own methods.
5. **Second-wave concepts** (the 10 face lines, then top tags by weighted reach) once a metric or page needs them.
6. **Sector templates**, banks first (1,339 companies with revenue but no gross profit).
7. **Financial Statement Data Sets** only after a size pilot (DB is 46 GB).

## Release tiers (mentor decision, because no accounting reviewer is named)
- **Auto-eligible after checks:** a single clean standard tag, exact match to the definition, full-population coexistence check shows no conflicting use, Verifier sample (12 cases, 30 if it feeds a locked metric or reaches 1,000+ companies) has 0 failures, applied by committed migration, with rollback and re-measure.
- **Proposal-only until a named human accounting reviewer approves:** anything that changes an accounting perimeter (APIC vs APIC-common, taxes Paid vs Net, preferred value, combined lines, fallbacks across tags), template/sector formulas, the existing `diluted_eps` / `net_income` priority-3 fallbacks (grandfather or revisit), and acceptance of the window-weight method for weighted-average shares.
- AI review never counts as accounting approval; labels say so.

## Decisions taken now (the Chief listed them as founder-only; made under delegation, reversible)
1. Headline KPI becomes the three-state ledger (value / proven-absent / unknown), not coverage %. Coverage % stays as a secondary number.
2. Foreign-private-issuer-only filers are outside the V1 operating denominator (already doc 02); the 83 companies with no 10-K stay in as UNKNOWN until diagnosed.
3. Derivation uses authoritative inputs only; conflicts resolve upstream first.
4. Restated periods store the latest-filed figure (decisions.md, 2026-10-18).
5. Financial Statement Data Sets: size pilot on one quarter first, no wider load without a measured size report.
6. Budget caps: batches of at most 10 rule families and 50 cases; model and Verifier time are capped per batch by those limits; cost telemetry is not available, so costs are reported as unknown, not guessed.
7. Coverage targets are NOT set until the fresh baseline exists.

## Still needs a person (cannot be decided by the team)
Name the accounting reviewer; adopt the written release policy (or keep batches proposal-only); set the acceptable wrong-value ceiling and freshness SLA; confirm that a transactional diff or company-subset replay can stand in for staging; confirm the sector scope the product promises at launch.

## Rules of engagement
`ListAgents` and a connection check before any heavy job; one connection per role task; `statement_timeout` set explicitly; schema changes only via committed migration (next number claimed in doc 53); read `git log` on a module before flagging it; commit named paths and push the same turn.

## Launch target (founder direction 2026-10-18: "all cells filled, and numbers correct")
"100% of cells filled" is rewritten as **100% accounted for**: every matrix cell is either (a) a correct value traceable to a filing, or (b) an explicit, reasoned non-value (not reported by the company / not applicable / not yet derivable, with the reason shown to the user). Filling a cell the company does not report would require a guess and would break the second half of the goal (correct numbers), so it is not done. Measured baseline 2026-10-13: 578,789 of 848,578 matrix cells have a value (68.2%); reasons are recorded for 22.8% of cells.
Launch-critical order: (1) stop wrong values (refresh_stale_fills, pending dry-run after the recompute); (2) fill gaps in the 33 concepts that feed metrics (EBITDA inputs, buyback inputs, incomplete numerator/add/subtract/denominator); (3) fill derivable quarters; (4) give every remaining blank a displayed reason; (5) Verifier sample against filings before launch. Founder direction 2026-10-18: launch date is ASAP, when ready; there is no acceptable wrong-value ceiling. Every displayed value is correct or visibly marked unavailable, and any wrong value the Verifier finds is fixed before launch (a sample size only measures what we have not found yet).

## Launch re-rank (Data Chief, 2026-10-18; replaces the "Order" list for a launch in days-to-weeks)
Corrections to my earlier statements: (a) Financial Statement Data Sets are NOT the biggest fill lever: they carry the same XBRL values as companyfacts, so they fill nothing companyfacts lacks (assumption, untested for older years); the lever is mapping vocabulary into the catalog (about 350 face lines cover most of what filers present), and FSDS only helps rank those and prove absence. Load stays a side pilot: download 2025q4 to local files, keep only sub/pre rows for our filers, report size, drop (a 500 MB cap is a proposal, not measured). (b) 10-K/A and 10-Q/A amendments ARE ingested (FORM_ALLOWLIST, restatements.py); the LGND error was a labeling error inside an amendment, so the fix is code detectors for mislabeled periods and full-year-under-quarter values, not a new form.
Days 1-3: the "authoritative same-tag copy wins" fix and the weighted-average reprocess (after the running recompute); fix the stuck nightly snapshot (stale since 2026-10-13) and the coverage report's covered>total bug (ROE 4,818 of 4,815); give every remaining blank a displayed reason; fix gaps in the 33 metric-feeding concepts (EBITDA inputs 7,817 cells, buyback inputs 5,652, incomplete numerator 23,790); Verifier sample against filings before launch.
Ship labelled instead of blank: "not reported by this company"; for banks, the FDIC slice (decisions.md 2026-10-18) or "not applicable to banks".
Cut or defer past launch: the 10 new face lines, FSDS load, 8-K Exhibit 99.1 checker, calculation/presentation linkbases, NT forms, long-tail tags, segments.
"No blanks" is defined as "no unexplained blanks" (see Launch target).
Needs the founder: launch date, wrong-value ceiling, a named accounting reviewer (also for the FDIC definitions).

## Correction 2026-10-18 (Verifier, filing-checked): do NOT derive operating income from Revenues minus CostsAndExpenses
The triage proposed it for 10 blank rows. The Verifier opened the filings: for CVX, AJG and HTZ the CostsAndExpenses total includes interest expense, so the subtraction equals pretax income, not operating income (CVX 0000093410-26-000078 R3; AJG 0001628280-26-008662 R3; HTZ 0001657853-26-000008 R7). Shipping it would label pretax income as operating income. All 10 rejected. Results of the 24-row filing check (`data-team/26-VERIFIER-24-GAP-BLANKS-2026-10-18.md`): 6 confirmed our gap (unmapped debt tags, one interest tag), 5 actually structural (no borrowings; vendor "Total Debt" was operating leases), 13 need a different definition first. Lever 1 above becomes a Standardization Lead decision (operating income when the statement shows no subtotal; SG&A as operating expense; zero-borrowings rule; net-interest-only filers), not a mapping job. Levers 2 and 3 (debt tags, CDNL interest expense) stay, gated on a full-population coexistence check after the recompute. Also found: NUTR (CIK 2006468) never filed a 10-K and filed Form 25-NSE on 2026-10-07 but is still `active`.

## Standardization rulings 2026-10-18 (proposal-only; no accounting reviewer named) - `data-team/27-STANDARDIZATION-DECISIONS-24-GAPS-2026-10-18.md`
- Operating income with no subtotal (CVX, AJG, HTZ): leave blank with reason "not reported as a subtotal by this company", shown only after a rendered-statement check. No derived number.
- SG&A is part of operating expenses, not the total; a separate revenue-minus-cost-of-revenue-minus-SG&A formula is proposal-only and needs an exact-match test against filers that tag operating income.
- Zero borrowings: only a reported zero (E1) or a filing statement of no borrowings (E2, proposal-only) may be 0; no debt line and no note (E3) stays blank. Operating leases are not in total_debt (confirmed); finance-lease treatment is inconsistent and is an accountant question.
- Net-interest-only filers (VVX, UNIT): no global mapping; interest coverage stays blank "only net interest reported".
- Debt tag fills are eligible only as a sole-tag last resort (NotesPayable, SeniorNotes, SubordinatedDebt, InterestExpenseNonoperating after check I-2); multi-tag sums, FHLB advances and Cash are proposal-only. CDNL's gross NotesPayable double-counts against its own net parts.
- DEFECT TO ROUTE (wrong-value class): the existing operating-income arithmetic fallback's own code comment says 10-15% of its fills miss the real figure by more than 2%. Under the zero-error standard this needs an audit and a fix or removal.
- Tag Mapper's read-only checks (D-1..D-6, I-1..I-5, Q-C, Q-D2, Q-D3b) are designed but not run; they wait for the recompute to finish.

## Stale-fill fix: DO NOT APPLY AS WRITTEN (Verifier, filing-checked, 2026-10-18) - `data-team/28-VERIFIER-STALE-FILL-PROPOSALS-2026-10-18.md`
Full-population dry-run proposed 4,755 replacements. Verifier opened original filings for 82 of an 87-row risk-stratified sample: proposed value wrong in 31 (and in 21 of 37 sign flips), stored value correct in 28, proposed correct in 34. Cause: the code matches stored and primary rows by company+period only, never the source tag; 64% of proposals substitute a different tag (a subset, a continuing-ops subtotal, a segment figure, an oppositely signed or zero tag), 282 are derived values mixing filing vintages, 255 come from 8-K / DEF 14A. The `authoritative_older_than_stored` filing-date guard cannot see any of these. Required before any write: (1) same source tag on both sides, (2) primary not derived, (3) primary from 10-K/10-Q/10-K/A/10-Q/A/10-KT only, (4) sign flips and zeros to review, (5) the refresh removed from the nightly (`resolve_all_conflict_fills`) until a reviewed dry-run of the guarded version. Guarded subset is about 1,015 rows; a clean sample of 25 there is not proof for the class. Own reviewed paths: 146 arithmetic operating-income rows (stored value omits cost of revenue); 1,189 ProfitLoss -> NetIncomeLoss rows (needs the Standardization Lead's call that net income means attributable to parent). Also routed: derived Q4 EPS/dividends-per-share and impossible-sign derived values (Period Engineer); ANDE ASC 606-only revenue, GE quarterly revenue = GE Capital only, zero/inverted tags at SHFH/MOJO/QWTR/BEEM/ARKO, CSUI total assets (Tag Mapper/Standardization); no detector for filer period-label and context errors (Data Engineer).

## Guarded stale-fill: still NOT safe as a class (Verifier, 146 of 570 proposals filing-checked) - `data-team/29-VERIFIER-GUARDED-STALE-FILL-2026-10-18.md`
19 of 146 would write a wrong value (1 of 20 random). The new guards worked (no different-tag swaps, derived values, proxy/8-K figures, sign flips). Five remaining patterns need guards: (1) later filing has a period-label error (SWK, CB, Ford, RDI, MHH, WYY): review when the proposed value equals the same company/tag/unit value at another period or the period is outside the filing's comparative window; (2) total cash-flow tag used for a continuing-ops subtotal (MGNC, LRDC): review when the primary filing also reports discontinued-ops cash flows; (3) cash vs cash-plus-restricted-cash under one tag (FDCT): review when restricted cash appears at the same date; (4) non-USD unit (IVDA, TWD): unit must be USD and equal the stored unit; (5) not a real period (EONR one-day interest): reject durations under 80 days and roughly annual durations sourced from a 10-Q. Plus: reverse-split EPS (22 of 31 EPS proposals; GURE, NCRA, SVMB, BURU) are never refreshed per cell, since our split table lacks those splits and a cell-by-cell change would mix post- and pre-split values in one company's history (guard: share count changes 1.5x or more between filings; route to split handling).
DECISION (Dhairya, under founder delegation 2026-10-18, reversible): this refresh path applies restatements and corrections only. Re-presentations (discontinued operations, spin-offs, perimeter changes; 13 of 20 random proposals) and reverse-split re-basing are whole-series changes with their own owners and are never refreshed cell by cell, because one re-presented year inside an original-basis series breaks growth, TTM and ratios. Narrowest supportable first subset: no detector flag and primary form 10-K/A or 10-Q/A (about 106 proposals; 38 sampled had no wrong row), still needing its own reviewed dry-run before any write.
Wrong values displayed today, routed: IVDA cash is the TWD component (Tag Mapper); segment-note operating income mapped as consolidated (AAME, DuPont, Kilroy); LGND's shifted 10-K/A (Period Engineer); missing splits for GURE, NCRA, SVMB, BURU (split owner).

## Sector templates for "every row filled" (Sector Analyst, proposal-only, DATA-INFERRED) - `data-team/30-SECTOR-TEMPLATES-FOR-FULL-ROWS-2026-10-19.md`
Families of 4,808 real operating companies and today's non-gated blank rows: banks/thrifts 326 (about 9,046), non-bank lenders/BDCs 148 (3,749), brokers/asset managers 107 (3,015), insurers 127 (2,998), REITs 196 (5,023), other real estate 69 (1,734), utilities 124 (2,188), energy 145 (2,954), transport 84 (1,339), pharma/biotech 710 (16,168; about 131 with no revenue value), everything else 2,686 (40,088), no SIC 54 (2,531). Top templates (row counts are upper bounds): (1) bank/thrift, about 20 rows per company, needs about 14 new concepts (net interest income, loan interest income, deposit interest expense, noninterest income/expense, provision, deposits, net/gross loans, allowance, nonaccrual, Tier 1); loans/allowance/provision switch tags at CECL adoption so need period-scoped tag sets; (2) pre-revenue overlay (R&D, cash burn, runway, dilution); (3) equity REIT/real estate (FFO/AFFO/NOI/occupancy stay "not in XBRL" with a reason); (4) insurer split by P&C/life/health/agents; (5) lenders/brokers/BDCs reusing bank concepts. Cross-cutting lever X1 (input fixes: debt tags, zero-borrowings, operating income, interest/cash tags; total_debt_resolved missing in about 1,800 of 2,200 blank ROIC cells) goes FIRST so templates sit on correct inputs; lever X2 (extend company_filing_line_evidence to every blank slot) is required before any "not applicable" reason may be shown.
URGENT, possible wrong values shown today (not fact-checked): banks show fcf_yield (209 of 326), debt_to_equity (210), net_cash; 15 banks show a net_interest_income computed as InvestmentIncomeInterest minus total interest expense, which is wrong for a bank; bank net interest income (InterestIncomeExpenseNet) maps only as priority 2 of other_income_expense_net. Bank revenue perimeter is undefined (total interest income vs NII + noninterest income vs Revenues) and every bank growth/margin row depends on it. Verifier to open about 30 stratified bank filings first.
DECISION (Dhairya, delegated, reversible): the 10-year CAGR rows keep the 2015 history gate; where history is not available the row shows the reason "history not available before XBRL" rather than a number. Pre-2011 values are not fabricated.

## Decisions on the Data Chief's three founder questions (Dhairya, delegated, reversible) - 2026-10-19, see `data-team/33-TOP1000-FIX-PLAN-2026-10-19.md`
1. Suppression first: ship the B1 serve-time guards now (they can only blank a value, never create a wrong one), accepting a lower fill rate. Stale values (older than the company's own latest filing by more than 1 fiscal year for annual, 2 quarters for quarterly/TTM) are HIDDEN with the reason "not in the latest filing (last reported FY...)", not shown with a stale label, because a visible old number beside current data is read as current.
2. Accounting reviewer: still not named. Until one is, these stay suppressed at launch: total_debt perimeter, 606-vs-total revenue, net income attributable to parent, financial revenue, capex definition, MLP equity. This is the one decision I cannot make: the constitution says AI review is not accounting approval.
3. Launch scope: the 90 non-bank financials, MLPs and multi-class/ADS issuers launch with explicit reasons, never with a value we know is wrong, and not delayed. A Q4 figure that exists only in the earnings release (KLAC $1.04) is NOT shown: the earnings release is furnished, not filed, and carries no XBRL (domain doc 21), so it cannot be traced to a filed fact.
Execution order: B0 (coverage honesty, the 10-Q source gap, proxy/8-K values) and B1 (guards) in parallel; B2/B3/B4 only after a dry-run plus Verifier sample with zero wrong rows each.

## Operating rule: no overlapping heavy jobs (scrooner-82, 2026-10-20)
The database restarted at 22:06Z on 2026-10-19 while the scheduled refresh (18:45Z), sanity (20:36Z) and SEC Frames (21:25Z) runs overlapped; overlapping scheduled jobs look like the trigger for the repeated restarts. Rules: (1) before any dispatch of a recompute, matrix rebuild, or local full-population job, check `gh run list` for in-progress runs and `pg_stat_activity`; (2) data-team agents run their full-population scans only between scheduled runs and never while a doc 57 D1/D2 run is in progress; (3) the heavy-job guard (`db/heavy_job_guard.py`, committed 03a89d2, not yet wired into commands) should be wired into `build-coverage-matrix`, `snapshot-coverage`, `resolve-conflict-fills` and the Tag Mapper scripts; (4) staggering the three scheduled workflows so they cannot overlap is a candidate fix for the Data Engineer after D2. Current holds: D1 run 39350145613 (started 00:51Z 2026-10-20), then D2; nothing heavy from the data team until scrooner-82 reports D2 ended.

## Overnight plan 2026-10-21 (Dhairya, founder asleep; resume from here if the session resets)
Goal: top-1000 certification (data-team/34). Running when this was written: Data Engineer building `scrooner-certify` (invariants I1-I10, migration claimed in doc 53 and NOT applied, dry-run on S001/S002); Tag Mapper total_debt/wrong-zero/interest checks on the top 1000 (file 31); Verifier on GE quarterly revenue context and a headline-cell trial (file 35).
Sequence as results arrive: (1) review and commit each agent's output after rerunning its tests myself; (2) from the certify dry-run, rank real wrong-value counts by invariant and feed rule families into data-team/33 batches; (3) first fix to build is whichever family the suite shows touches the most top-1000 companies with FILING-CHECKED evidence: expected total_debt composition, net income from proxy/8-K (check whether doc 57 D2 ever ran), stuck 10-Q ingestion; (4) every fix: dry-run, Verifier sample with zero wrong rows, committed code or migration, only then any DB write or recompute; (5) migrations are committed, NOT applied, until a human or scrooner-82 style gate confirms no heavy run is in flight; (6) hold rule: no recompute, matrix rebuild or full-population job while `gh run list` shows an in-progress run or pg_stat_activity shows over 14 connections; (7) if a usage limit hits, resume agents with SendMessage after it resets.
Nothing in this plan writes to the production database without a reviewed dry-run. Morning report must list: certified companies by state, wrong values found by the suite, fixes committed, what is blocked on a human (named accounting reviewer for debt perimeter, 606-vs-total revenue, net income attributable to parent, financial revenue, capex definition).

## Who does what (clarified 2026-10-21 after the founder asked why all execution happens in the chief of staff's session)
Subagents cannot spawn subagents, so one orchestrator dispatches. That is Dhairya (chief of staff): decides, dispatches, communicates, escalates. NOT hands-on: queries, test reruns, merges, pushes. Roles: Head of Data Product = strategy and priorities; Data Chief = ranked execution plans; Standardization Lead, Tag Mapper, Tag Expert, Period Engineer, Data Engineer, Sector Analyst = build; Data Verifier = filing checks; Data Triage = queue; Release Manager (data-team/08) = tests, commit, push; the top-1000 campaign session = finds problems. From now on completed agent work goes to the Release Manager, not through the chief of staff's own shell.

## SEC fetch rule (Dhairya, delegated, reversible; 2026-10-22, from the Data Head review)
Replaces 'one SEC fetch process at a time, at most 2 requests per second' for the data team: up to THREE concurrent SEC fetch processes on this machine with a COMBINED cap of 5 requests per second (SEC fair access is a per-IP limit well above this), always with the declared SEC_USER_AGENT from pipeline settings, honouring 429/403 backoff; bulk files (FSDS/FSN) are streamed and deleted after reading (stop if Mac free disk < 3 GB). Each agent states in its report how many requests it made. If the SEC throttles any process, drop to one process and report.

## SEC fetch rule, amended by scrooner-4d (2026-10-22): supersedes the earlier text
Up to three concurrent SEC fetch processes on this Mac with a combined cap of 5 requests per second is allowed ONLY if every process uses the shared cross-process (flock) rate limiter already in the pipeline (`CrossProcessRateLimiter`; aggregate cap 8 per host). The CI/runner and this Mac have SEPARATE IP budgets: do not start a Mac bulk fetch (FSDS/FSN streaming) while the Mac is running a pipeline fetch. Agents that fetch with ad hoc curl scripts must route through the same limiter or fall back to one process at a time.

(yfinance); AvalonBay id 1895 still active/AVB though delisted (25-NSE 2026-08-17) and deregistered (15-12G 2026-08-27, 15-15D 2026-09-16); no ticker history (no EQR in core.listing); market cap 23.3B uses pre-merger 374.9M shares, post-merger about 773.7M (about 48.1B, to be confirmed on the Q3 10-Q); the 424B3 (0001140361-26-028312) says a reverse acquisition with AvalonBay as accounting acquirer, so from the Q3 2026 10-Q (about early November) this CIK's comparatives are expected to be AvalonBay's history: growth and TTM would mix two companies; needs a founder/accounting decision on the history basis BEFORE that filing is ingested.
Cohort identity: 1 name disagreement (PSKY id 4474 is now Skydance Corp, ticker SKYD, 8-K 0001104659-26-113913, effective 2026-10-06); WBD (5851) acquired, 15-12G 2026-10-16, status still active; EAI shows a bond name as display name. Form 15 alone is not a delisting signal (BMY, APO preferred; PG plan interests; RKLB predecessor's common).
Entergy/registrant mechanism: one instance, 7 registrants; undimensioned facts belong to the default entity (dei:EntityCentralIndexKey 0000065984); co-registrants are LegalEntityAxis members; SEC companyfacts keeps only undimensioned facts and may serve them under a co-registrant's CIK (Q2 2026 under Entergy Arkansas 7323; likewise Sempra->SoCalGas, Southern->Southern Co Gas, Edison->SCE). OUR bug (code-read): normalizer/identity.py upserts filings on conflict (accession_number) without updating company_id (first CIK processed keeps a shared accession); normalizer/facts.py _load_filing_lookup assumes a filing belongs to one company, so parents' facts on shared accessions are dropped as skipped_no_filing. Result: Entergy Corp (694) has no facts after 2010, status stale, no ticker; Entergy Arkansas (142) holds 467 Entergy Corp facts; its 8.5B market cap = Entergy Corp shares x the price of a 4.875% 2066 bond. SAME PATTERN: 9 parents gone stale with no ticker: SO, SRE, AEE, ETR, CTVA, EIX, CMS, ES, TXNM; therefore the 986 cohort is mis-drawn. Rule candidate P1: for an accession whose header lists 2+ filers, the primary filer is the CIK in the instance's undimensioned dei:EntityCentralIndexKey, cross-checked against the first header filer (and FSDS sub.txt where available); disagreement quarantines the accession. Agreed on 9 of 9 test accessions; accession prefix wrong on 2; companyfacts wrong on 4 of 7. Second independent reader: 3 readers (regex R-file, lxml iXBRL, edgartools 5.49.0) on 5 filings, 309 rows: all agree; member rows (166) are the regex reader's blind spot.

## Release Manager review (2026-10-22): SIGNED WITH CONDITIONS - data-team/strategy/team/WO-REVIEW-release-manager.md; working-tree rule (Dhairya, delegated)
Conditions: (1) WO-9 text corrected (the revenue-total guard does not exist; RU-1 and the total-debt guards sit on non-main branches held/ru1-per-share and held/total-debt-guards; never-derive-Q4-EPS exists only inside RU-1 and derived.py is never released alone); (2) before the first value-changing train: the flag (default OFF), a per-family dry-run mode, and a before-image/restore script exist; (3) per-agent worktrees adopted and the shared tree salvaged first; (4) migration claims written in doc 53 on origin before the Release Manager commits their files; (5) the Release Manager never dispatches or cancels workflows (scrooner-4d and Dhairya do); (6) Gate B reconciled with the post-R4 certify; the calendar acknowledges 4 trains (R1-R4), not 3: Gate B (Fri 11-13) cannot include the post-R4 certify, which finishes after midnight UTC. Value-changing release procedure (R2/R3), 10 steps: preconditions by script (sign-offs, rule ID, consumer count, flag name, rollback line, slot row in doc 53, migration claim on origin); code on a branch with the flag OFF + unit + real-SQL scratch-schema + mutation tests; dry-run in a heavy slot dispatched by scrooner-4d; Verifier V60+15+15 with zero wrong rows (correct values hidden reported separately; sampler is not the proposer); commit to main from an origin worktree with the flag OFF + rollback line + revert command + ciks + backup path; before-image saved under backups/ and restore rehearsed on 10 ciks; separate first live run on 10 companies with the flag enabled only by a pipeline-recompute.yml dispatch input (not a repo variable, which would reach the nightly); full live run reconciled to the dry-run; a tiny second commit flips the default ON before the next 12:23Z nightly; certify re-run shows no new finding class. Refusals: any family with no flag / no dry-run summary / no signed sample / no rollback line / default ON; RU-1 in pieces; total-debt guards without persisted reasons, a 2% hide-only floor, and filing-checked overcount cases.
WORKING-TREE HAZARD (measured): local main 96 behind / 8 ahead of origin; 2 local-only commits (9b3e624, 746b80f) would be lost by a reset; 102 status entries (57 tracked files modified, +4,890/-639) mixing five release units; .githooks/ missing from the shared tree so pushes from it run no hook. ## F-3 growth-base proposal (Standardization Lead, 2026-10-22; DATA-INFERRED, code read only, no DB, no filing opened)
Finding: mapper/ttm.py::_compute_growth_for_company (L340-401) computes growth for every (fiscal_year, fiscal_period) key as (fy - lag, same fp): pure label arithmetic, no date/duration check. The 1,776 `missing:prior_period` blanks with 2016+ quarter bases come from this; it also writes growth on Q1-Q3 rows. The screener (screener/resolve.py L100-103) takes the TTM row, else the latest period_end whatever its label, and the engine writes no TTM growth rows, so a screener "10Y revenue CAGR" can be a single-quarter figure or a blank even when a valid FY CAGR exists (NOT measured). Company page reads FY rows only. PEG (expanded_metrics.py L635) may read quarterly eps_growth_yoy (unchecked).
Proposed: growth only on FY (350-380 day) bases chosen by DATE (end within +/-14 days of t.end minus lag years), optional TTM, never Q1-Q3 rows; EPS/DPS FY only (no derivation, DPS guard FY >= each filed quarter); shares growth is a DEFINITION defect (shares_outstanding is period-end common shares, not diluted weighted average: rename or new concept); fcf_growth.py `limit 1` with no order by is nondeterministic (10-KT / FYE change); F-2: base period_start >= 2015-01-01, else state history_window_insufficient (first 10-year CAGR FY2025 for Dec filers). Risks: 52/53-week (+/-1.9% annual, 7.7% on quarters), restatement basis across filings (606, discontinued ops, reverse acquisition, de-SPAC), splits, two-loss CAGR producing a positive number (Ford +44.9%), mixed vintage TTM.
Needs accountant: (a) multi-year endpoint restatement policy, (b) 53-week treatment, (c) DPS declared vs paid, (d) shares-growth definition, (e) show growth across two losses or not. No accountant needed: date-based matching, dropping Q1-Q3 growth, tolerance windows, screener/PEG row selection, fcf_growth determinism. Founder call: does a June-FYE FY2015 count for F-2 (proposed rule: base period_start >= 2015-01-01).
Routing: Data Engineer growth lane (ttm.py, fcf_growth.py, screener/PEG selection, dry-run of delete/insert diff); accountant cards (a)-(e); impact query plan (4 counts, NOT_RUN) before any change.

## Rulings on lane 2 hand-back (Data Chief brief, adopted by Dhairya 2026-10-22; code-read, nothing applied)
1. Merge before Gate A: branches 6 (ledger notes), 3 (ingestion detector, read-only), 5 (I12/I13 reserve, after rebase) MAY go to main in the next Release Manager batch (publication currently blocked pending the founder's push permission). Branches 1 (RF-01/02) and 2 (W10) WAIT: per doc 49 line 69 "committed" means on main with flag OFF after the dry-run and Verifier sample (V 60+15+15); dry-run runs on the branch ref in a heavy slot by scrooner-4d after the four workflows are green and the founder's go reaches it.
2. BLOCKER: two incompatible flag modules share SCROONER_RULES_ENABLED (lane 1 common/rule_flags.py raises ValueError on unknown ids; lane 2 mapper/rule_flags.py accepts them). Before either value-changing branch merges: ONE module in common/ with one combined known-rule list and off/dry_run/on modes (lane 1 owns flag plumbing per doc 49). Lane 2 W10 must split off branch 1 or the shared pieces (flags, reason_store stub, before-image tool) move to a no-behaviour branch. Still missing: pipeline-recompute.yml dispatch input, flag-on-then-restore rehearsal, doc 49 flag-OFF byte-identical golden test.
3. expanded_metrics TTM rows per compute date (DATA-INFERRED on companies 225, 1, 89: 10-12 rows per metric for ebitda, buyback_yield, total_shareholder_yield): HIGH as a blocker for Tier 0 hides and Gate A measurement, LOW for storage. A guard hides by writing a newer null row but older valued rows stay; certification/loader.py (~L406) takes the latest NON-null TTM row, so certify keeps judging a value users no longer see; has_value_any_period would count hidden cells as valued forever; the cell key changes per compute date; period_end = compute date blinds common/staleness.py (the W2 mechanism). Owner: lane 2. Fix: delete prior TTM rows for OUTPUT_METRIC_NAMES per company in the same transaction as the insert (as price_metrics does), per-company advisory lock; one-time historical cleanup by committed migration with archive and reviewed dry-run. Until then certify's metric loader is not trusted for these metrics.
4. total_shareholder_yield hidden when a component is stale: CONFIRMED and REQUIRED (with RF-02 ON a stale TTM becomes None and the legacy branch would price it 0, creating a W8-type wrong value). Register as an RF-02 consumer; reason names every stale component; a component counts as 0 only with a current TTM or a filing-evidenced zero (CMI 0000026172-26-000009 R6 pattern; PFE W8 0000078003-26-000095 R8); share one rule with W8 (R2 in doc 49).
5. RF-02 200-day parameter: replace with ttm.age_guard@2, a same-period rule: TTM anchor end must equal the expected period end (latest periodic filing period_of_report via staleness.latest_filed_period_sql), tolerance 7 days, missing reference = hidden (fail closed), composites need all components on the same window end. Dry-run to print the lag histogram (expect bunches at 0 and ~91/182 days); Verifier classifies the ~91/~182 bunches by filing. The three thresholds (200, QUARTERLY_MAX_GAP_DAYS 230, TTM_STALENESS_THRESHOLD_DAYS 150) should read one definition until F6 current_quarter() lands.
6. W10 share-date fallback: a census of all 27 companies with only a cover-dated count (cheaper than a sample), plus a ~30-company control set of the 167 filing both dates to set the tolerance. Proposed rule pershare.share_count_date@1: A use the balance-sheet-date count; B cover-dated count only if same accession, cover date after balance-sheet date and <= filing date, no split/share-series step between, no issuance/buyback above tolerance, classes complete (labelled "shares as of <cover date>"); C otherwise incomplete:denominator. B changes the definition (two dates in one ratio): Standardization Lead decision card first, Period Engineer supplies evidence, Verifier re-reads.

ADS ratios (ordinary shares per ADS), FILING-CHECKED from cover text (dei:Security12bTitle): AKTX 80,000 since 2026-03-31 (10-Q 0001493152-26-037730; 10-K cover still said 2,000, so a ratio read from the latest filing was wrong 2026-03-31 to 2026-08-13; stored cap $1.364T vs ~$17.06M), ZLAB 10 (10-Q 0001628280-26-053793; $27.16B vs ~$2.61B; 1-for-10 split 2022-03-30), SCNI 4,000 (20-F 0001213900-26-038011; $8.27B vs ~$2.09M), KZIA 500 (20-F 0001193125-25-272284; $6.62B vs ~$13.2M), DBVT 5 (10-Q 0001628280-26-048453; $2.88B vs ~$573M less treasury), GPCR 3 (10-Q 0001104659-26-092057; $4.75B vs ~$1.58B), AMRN 20 (10-Q 0001193125-26-323845; $4.83B vs ~$241.5M), plus SLN 3 (10-Q 0001193125-26-341248; $2.78B vs ~$928M), NOT on the hold list. TEVA (ratio 1) negative control.
Findings the lane 3 guard does not handle: P/E (built from EPS per ordinary share) and dividend yield stay visible and wrong (stored P/E DATA-INFERRED: AMRN -164.3 should be -8.2, ZLAB -134.4, GPCR -22.0, DBVT -13.7, SLN -44.6); the 200x scale test cannot catch ADS errors (all share counts are in ordinary shares and agree; detection must come from the cover text; ADR listing type set for only 8 companies). RPAY and TBLA are FILER errors (share counts printed in full units tagged as thousands; printed EPS proves it: RPAY (10,986)K / 83,285,379 = (0.13); TBLA 63,383K / 280,185,111 = 0.23) that propagate because _choose_shares prefers the weighted-average count over the cover count when they differ by more than 25%; RPAY Class A 89,860,647 x $4.16 = $373.8M with Class V (100 voting-only shares) never counted; TBLA voting 252,115,754 + non-voting 18,039,644 = $921.2M subject to the accountant. KZIA and SCNI file 20-F yet sit in real_operating_company (foreign filers out of V1 scope). AMRN's ADR-class cover fact (20,632,560) is by arithmetic an ADS count labelled ordinary shares (unit ASSUMED): never use. DBVT cover count includes treasury shares.
Method card: NA-15 ADS conversion (parse ratio from cover text; hide when the ratio changed since that filing; market cap = ordinary shares / ratio x ADS price; per-share EPS/dividends get the same ratio). NA-16 class sum (per-class cover counts from the filing itself since companyfacts strips the class dimension; each listed class its own price; convert between classes only with a ratio the filing states, e.g. BRK 1/1,500; weighted averages never used as market-cap shares). Tests T1-T12.
Accountant: ADS-card items numbered AC-22..AC-25 in the file COLLIDE with the growth cards AC-22..AC-26 already issued (F-3): RENUMBER the ADS cards to AC-27 (Up-C exchangeable units, RPAY 5,285,883), AC-28 (pre-funded warrants: GPCR, AKTX, DBVT), AC-29 (pricing an unlisted class that converts into a listed one: TBLA non-voting, GOOGL Class B), AC-30 (treasury shares in the cover count: DBVT). Founder F-4: may a filer scale error proven by the filing's own EPS be corrected for one company, or only hidden? Data Chief: foreign-filer scope for KZIA and SCNI. Data Engineer: add SLN to ADS_HOLDS; hide trailing_pe and dividend_yield for any ads_* reason (live hook and dry run); per-listing prices; per-class cover reader and census table (claim migration numbers in doc 53); then NA-15 from the ratios. Verifier: independent check of the ratio table, starting with AMRN's ADR-fact unit and DBVT's treasury count. Not tested: ratio history before the dates in the table, how many other companies have the ADS problem (needs the census), whether the Alpaca bar is the ADS quote, SPCX.

## Period Engineer W10 share-date census (2026-10-22): COMPLETE read-only; data-team/strategy/team/W10-SHARE-DATE-CENSUS-2026-10-22.md and data/w10_share_date_census_2026-10-22.csv (98 rows) written to the shared tree, uncommitted
Recommendation: do NOT adopt clause B (cover-date fallback) as an automatic rule; most cover-only companies are capture gaps, so widen clause A. The probe on the same seed-1 sample of 300 (bs_anchor_dryrun output matches lane 2's w10_s300.csv exactly) found 232 companies with a cover-dated count after the latest balance-sheet date, 193 also with a balance-sheet-date count, and 39 (not 27) with only the cover count (38 match the dry-run's incomplete:denominator; the 39th is AIV). Lane 2's 194/167/27 query is not in the branch: LANE 2 MUST STATE IT so the counts reconcile.
Census of the 39 (FILING-CHECKED from the XBRL instances plus EDGAR filing lists; all 10-Qs; cover fact in the same accession, 13-45 days after the balance-sheet date, on/before filing date): 30 capture gaps (18 issued minus treasury, both undimensioned at the balance-sheet date, e.g. KO 7,040M - 2,737M, within 0.91% of cover; 7 one class filed only with a class dimension: RGR, WCC, VTSI, WTW, RENT, SNFI, HOVR; 3 issued count with no treasury fact: MDU, TROW, SRTA [issued = outstanding DATA-INFERRED]; 2 two classes to sum: PRLD, SEV, where the cover counts only listed Class B 28,560,115 and the balance sheet also has Class A 11,983,010, so a cover fallback would silently drop a class); 5 with no balance-sheet-date outstanding count anywhere (HUBB, LEG, HOG, NSP, PKOH); 4 out of scope (AIV liquidation-basis accounting from 2026-02-01; Athene all shares held by Apollo; DBVT and NNDM ADS issuers, NNDM ASSUMED). TRAP (FILING-CHECKED): at PKOH, CSW, DK, ALOT and USPH the equity-statement common-stock share column is the ISSUED count although tagged outstanding (PKOH 18,575,430 vs 14,490,010 at the cover date = 28% too high).
Control set (192 companies filing both dates; gap = |cover - BS|/BS, DATA-INFERRED): median 0.043%, p90 7.5%, 35 above 1%, 23 above 5% (excluding SPACs 30 of 187 above 1%); sample 29 (seed random.Random(20261022)) plus the whole >1% tail = 59 filing-checked. What moves the tail: 5 SPACs (BS count excludes redeemable public shares), 2 mergers (ZSTK, ESQ), 24 issuances/conversions/unexplained (IVR sold 5,380,335 shares via ATM after the balance-sheet date, no 8-K; BDPT 36.6M -> 196.9M via debt conversions, no 8-K, subsequent-events note "No significant event to report"), 3 buybacks (ABR, LITS, BZH), 1 reverse split between the dates (VISM 1-for-1,500). No tested signal substitutes for the missing count: the EDGAR event screen misses 9 of 35 >1% cases; the weighted-average check catches 25 of 27 but misses BZH and TGL with 58 false alarms of 147.
Proposed pershare.share_count_date@2 (all same filing, same balance-sheet date): A1 today's undimensioned outstanding; A2 sum of all common classes only if every class present and none redeemable; A3 issued minus treasury, undimensioned (needs its own decision card); A4 issued only when the filing has no treasury line at all (needs one more treasury-dollar-line check); never read the equity-statement column unless it equals A3. Cover count = cross-check only (review flag above 5%, never a value). Everything else -> C with reason incomplete:denominator:no_balance_sheet_date_share_count, plus not-applicable reasons for AIV and Athene. DATA-INFERRED effect on this sample: of 35 in-scope, 26 recovered, 3 more once A4 is checked, HOVR waits on a ruling about a "to be issued" share line, 5 stay blank with a reason.
Routed, not fixed: SPAC book value per share on the W10 path divides by founder shares only (definition question, Standardization Lead); PKOH issued-as-outstanding tagging; AIV rows carry the wrong reason class; NNDM and TGL weighted-average share counts have a scale/sign error; AVLN cover date is after its filing date. Not tested: beyond the 300 sample, 10-K filers, rendered R-file pages, the A4 treasury-line condition, PRLD/HOVR cover text, SGOL (instance did not parse), 8-K documents themselves.
Next: lane 2 states its probe query; Standardization Lead writes the decision card for A2/A3/A4 and the SPAC book-value definition; Verifier re-reads the 5 residual companies and the 5 equity-column traps; Period Engineer runs the probe population-wide in a quiet slot once approved.

## Tag Expert sector tag run (2026-10-22): COMPLETE; read-only DB (one connection, 20s, 8-11 backends) + 33 SEC requests; cards under data-team/tags/ written in the shared tree, uncommitted
1. The NetInvestmentIncome hypothesis is REFUTED. The 24 insurers in bdc_total_investment_income come from us-gaap:GrossInvestmentIncomeOperating (concept 205 has exactly one mapping, priority 1, approved, first_match; NetInvestmentIncome is unmapped everywhere). All 1,099 source facts of the 24 use the gross tag (10-Q 722, 10-K 376, 10-K/A 1). For insurers it is the gross investment-income note line before investment expenses (FILING-CHECKED: CINF 0000020286-26-000008 note "Total" 1,182M less expenses 17M = face NII 1,165M; TRV 0000086312-26-000065; PRU 0001137774-26-000048). Across 46 insurers gross minus investment expense equals NII in 1,528 of 1,683 shared periods (DATA-INFERRED). Of the tag's 118 filers only 42 are BDCs (47 SIC-63xx insurers, 12 SIC-62xx staking ETPs and KKR, 4 SIC 6411, 13 others). WIDER IMPACT (code read): mapper/coverage_matrix.py defines the bdc_company population as "has any bdc_total_investment_income value", so 76 non-BDCs sit in the BDC denominator; the 2026-09-04 note in mapper/expanded_concepts.py (~L418) "118 BDCs plus other closed-end funds" is false. NetInvestmentIncome: BDCs = pre-tax line (ARCC 0001287750-26-000006: 1,458M before taxes, tax 43M, after-tax 1,415M on InvestmentIncomeOperatingAfterExpenseAndTax; differ in 445 of 881 shared BDC periods); insurers = face NII line; never pool the two groups under one concept.
2. B3 bank interest expense: banks moved from InterestExpense to InterestExpenseOperating from 2021-09 (308 of 326 active banks file the latter, 26 still the former; equal in 2,222 of 2,254 shared periods; the 32 differences are filing vintages: CIVB 2022 11,049K in its 10-K/A vs 15,951K in its FY2024 10-K R4). Period-scoped pair. LIVE DEFECT LEAD: interest_expense maps only InterestExpense (p1) and InterestExpenseDebt (p2); EFSC has FY2024 282,955K and no FY2025 row although its FY2025 10-K (0001025835-26-000058) shows 261,672K on InterestExpenseOperating. Full-population effect unchecked (only EFSC).
3. B10/B12 CECL era break: net loans LoansAndLeasesReceivableNetReportedAmount (median last period 2022-12) -> FinancingReceivable...AfterAllowance... (224 banks current); allowance LoansAndLeasesReceivableAllowance -> ...AllowanceForCreditLossExcludingAccruedInterest; shared periods agree 94-98%. MISUSE: at OSBC (0001558370-23-008865) the undimensioned post-CECL net-loans tag (50,478K) is the collateral-dependent loans note total while face "Net loans" (3,820,129K) stays on the old tag. Need a period-scoped set plus a check that each value is the face net-loans line. LoansReceivableNet is a removed historical element (143 companies to 2020-03): keep for old periods. AllowanceForLoanAndLeaseLosses* elements are roll-forward components, never the allowance balance.
4. BDCs: F2 confirmed (InvestmentIncomeOperatingAfterExpenseAndTax first for after-tax NII; exists only from 2020-09, pre-2020 open). F3 partly wrong: StockholdersEquity is the face line (ARCC 14,318M), but 31 of 42 BDCs file AssetsNet, equal to equity in 487 of 568 shared periods: alternate after an equality check (DATA-INFERRED; TSLX latest negative).
5. Utilities: face = RegulatoryAssetsCurrent + Noncurrent (CPK 0001628280-26-011753: 29.7M + 74.3M); plain RegulatoryAssets is the note TOTAL (104.0M), equal to Current+Noncurrent in 590 of 629 periods and matches Noncurrent alone in only 73 of 707: alternate total, never the noncurrent line. Regulatory liabilities not population-checked.
6. Managed care: merge into insurer rows I1/I4 (UNH 0000731766-26-000062: "Premiums" 352,229M on PremiumsEarnedNet; "Medical costs" 313,995M on PolicyholderBenefitsAndClaimsIncurredNet). The draft health tags cover history only (...IncurredHealthCare deprecated 2024, last fact 2024-03; HealthCareOrganizationPremiumRevenue removed, 3 companies to 2018).
7. Crypto: CryptoAssetNoncurrent has never been an element. MSTR (0001050446-26-000020 R3): plain CryptoAssetFairValue = face "Digital assets" 58.85B. CLSK (0001193125-26-039538): plain tag 144,163K is a narrative subset beside face current 830,073K + noncurrent 171,924K (matches Current+Noncurrent in only 11 of 33 shared periods). Gain/loss set needs gain-only and loss-only unrealized tags (MSTR uses CryptoAssetUnrealizedLossOperating) and the nonoperating tag. Data from 2022 on only.
8. METHOD CORRECTION: SEC Frames counts are LOWER BOUNDS (EFSC's companyconcept fact is labelled frame CY2025 yet the CY2025 frame omits EFSC; the frame lists 56 entities vs 538 active companies in our tag library). The interest card's "15 filers" figure was wrong for this reason; other Frames counts in revenue.md and cash.md are now known to be lower bounds but not re-counted.
Cards: new cards/investment-income-and-funds.md, bank-loans-and-allowance.md, insurance-and-managed-care.md, utility-regulatory-and-crypto.md; changed cards/interest.md (Frames count, era rule, ARCC "after fees" corrected to pre-tax, EFSC blank), INDEX.md (3 rows corrected, 11 added), company-quirks.md (+10), sources.md, feedback-inbox.md (S2 line processed, +5), CHANGELOG.md; 5 ledger rows; one mind inbox entry. ## Standardization Lead decision card: pershare.share_count_date@2 (2026-10-22): PROPOSAL ONLY, not verified or approved; data-team/strategy/team/DC-SHARE-COUNT-DATE-2026-10-22.md (shared tree, uncommitted)
Scope: one periodic-form accession, at the balance-sheet date, consolidated, common shares only. Order A1 -> A2 -> A3 -> A4; else blank `incomplete:denominator:no_balance_sheet_date_share_count`. Rules on every path: R1 two computable paths must agree within rounding or the cell is held; R2 the equity-statement column is never a value source (corroborates only when it equals A3); R3 cover count is a cross-check only (above 5% the cell is held for review, value stays the balance-sheet-date count; 5% is a review trigger, not an accepted error rate); R4 never weighted-average, SharesIssued, TemporaryEquitySharesOutstanding, pre-funded-warrant or "to be issued" members, ADR members; R5 a derived count keeps the coarser rounding of its inputs. Structural exits before the paths: liquidation basis (AIV), no public common (Athene), SPAC redeemable common, ADS, non-USD equity.
A2 class sum: every class has a count (a filed zero counts); only classes with class-level EPS, equal EPS, none redeemable; RPAY Class V excluded (100 shares, no EPS fact); BRK hidden unless the stated 1/1,500 conversion applies; no accountant for equal-rights classes (AC-22 Up-C units and AC-24 unequal/unlisted classes stay with the accountant). A3 issued minus treasury: legal definition of outstanding at one date, not a period subtraction, no accountant (AC-25 subsidiary/trust-held shares open); both facts undimensioned, same date, negative treasury quarantined; fixes the equity-column traps (CSW 16,301,000; DK 61,199,218). A4 issued only: only when the filing has no treasury line of any kind (MDU, TROW, SRTA pass, FILING-CHECKED; HOG, NSP, PKOH refused: each has a treasury dollar line).
Acceptance (FILING-CHECKED, instances opened): KO A3 = 7,040,000,000 - 2,737,000,000 = 4,303,000,000 (equity column agrees; cover 0.012% off; publishes); SEV A2 = 11,983,010 + 25,439,653 = 37,422,663 (cover counts only listed Class B 28,560,115, 23.7% off: held, never takes the cover number); PRLD A2 = 65,028,996 + 14,728,135 = 79,757,131 (never the weighted average 98,388,593 which includes pre-funded warrants); PKOH blank (only share fact is the equity column 18,575,430 = issued; treasury dollar column exists); HUBB blank (no share-unit fact at the balance-sheet date).
SPAC book value per share: founder shares only is WRONG TO DISPLAY (GUAC: -10,484,793 / 7,877,500 = -1.33; its 31,510,000 public shares, 80% of common, sit outside equity at $10.04 redemption value). The fix also applies to the CURRENT A1 path, not only the new paths. Default until the accountant rules on new item AC-SC-1 (renumber to AC-31 to avoid collisions): not_applicable:redeemable_common_in_temporary_equity; the alternative (equity + temporary equity) / all shares = 7.77 is a non-GAAP choice for the accountant.
HOVR: the "to be issued" line (8,204 shares for services) is not outstanding, count 67,123,941 (matches market cap's outstanding-only definition, AC-23; PRLD pre-funded warrants same). HOVR stays blank anyway: statements are in CAD but canonical stockholders_equity holds facts 73183954 and 216945757 with unit `cad` as if USD (filing FILING-CHECKED; stored state DATA-INFERRED): reason unit:non_usd_numerator. This is a WRONG-VALUE CLASS to scan population-wide (money concepts stored with a non-USD unit). Second defect: BVPS numerator is total stockholders' equity (mapper/expanded_definitions.py), so preferred (HOVR 6,277K) is included; a common-equity definition card is needed. ADS issuers: share rule unchanged, display waits on NA-15; interim reason ads_basis_unconverted.
Not tested: 10-K filers, beyond the census sample, rendered R-files, holdings at A4 companies not called treasury (assumed none), redeemable preferred filed without a class member (could falsely trigger the SPAC rule), CSW/DK/ALOT/USPH (relied on the census), BRK (tag card). ## Standardization Lead: common-equity BVPS card + AC renumber (2026-10-22): PROPOSAL ONLY (not verified or approved); data-team/strategy/team/DC-COMMON-EQUITY-BVPS-2026-10-22.md (shared tree, uncommitted)
Renumber done: the SPAC accountant item is AC-31 (earlier PLAN.md text may still say AC-SC-1). Share-count card section 5 (SPAC hide trigger) revised after a filing check: STKS (0001104659-26-092751) files its redeemable preferred count of 160,000 twice (untagged and preferred-tagged); the old trigger "untagged and > 0" would have hidden STKS by mistake; the trigger now fires only when the redeemable count is not fully explained by preferred-tagged facts, and hides if the class cannot be identified.
Numerator: new concept common_equity_resolved = parent stockholders' equity minus preferred stock in permanent equity; USD only; same filing and balance-sheet date as the share count; BVPS = this / share_count_date@2 count. Parent equity: first choice plain StockholdersEquity; second choice the NCI-inclusive total-equity tag minus MinorityInterest at the same date (if MinorityInterest is absent, allowed only with filing evidence that NCI is zero, else hidden); cross-check against a filing-tagged parent-equity figure; never the NCI-inclusive total alone as parent equity. Preferred: PreferredStockValue, PreferredStockValueOutstanding, PreferredStockIncludingAdditionalPaidInCapital(NetOfDiscount) or the equity statement's preferred column (line and column must agree; per-series amounts sum to the total); zero only with filing evidence; carrying amount, not liquidation preference (JPM FILING-CHECKED: (374,598M - 21,040M) / 2,658,186,195 = 133.007, matching JPM's printed $133.01; liquidation basis 132.95); dividends in arrears hidden pending AC-32; redeemable preferred in temporary equity already outside equity (STKS, LMNR); redeemable NCI ignored (CRL, UNH, HIW OP units); NCI in equity excluded (DHI, UNH, PSA, CRL, HIW); Up-C/UpREIT stays with AC-22; SPACs with AC-31; participating preferred hidden pending NEW AC-33.
Acceptance (filings opened): JPM common equity 353,558M, BVPS 133.007 vs STORED 140.92 (5.9% TOO HIGH, a wrong value shown today); PSA 4,840,280K (preferred is 47% of equity, so today's formula nearly doubles BVPS; no recent stored value); HIW 2,364,809K / 110,306,211 = 21.439; UNH 104,513M - 6,066M NCI = 98,447M (no parent-equity fact or subtotal on the face) vs stored 115.48 (includes NCI); DHI 23,819.5M; CRL 2,839,636K; STKS -92,074K, -2.906 per share (stored matches); HOVR 61,380K but CAD, so hidden. Wider issue to route: the NCI-inclusive fallback also feeds ROE and leverage through canonical stockholders_equity (needs its own card; in SEC's Q4 2025 statement data set 120 balance sheets carry that tag plus MinorityInterest but no plain StockholdersEquity, DATA-INFERRED). One DB problem: a population-wide candidate search timed out and later hung; the agent killed its own local process and confirmed the server-side query ended; the impact count is NOT measured (the card states a query plan). Not tested: 10-K filers, dividends in arrears, participating preferred, preferred carried below liquidation preference, R-files, population impact counts.
Next: Verifier re-reads both cards against the filings; accountant rules on AC-31, AC-32, AC-33; Standardization Lead writes the card replacing the stockholders_equity fallback (ROE/leverage); Data Engineer adds a USD-only check on money concepts; impact count in a heavy-job slot or from the SEC statement data files.

## Tag Mapper: interest_expense + BDC gate (2026-10-22): COMPLETE read-only; all mapping rows are PROPOSALS; data-team/strategy/team/TAG-MAPPER-INTEREST-EXPENSE-AND-BDC-GATE-2026-10-22.md (shared tree, uncommitted); 20s, 10 backends, ~20 SEC R-file fetches
Task 1 InterestExpenseOperating (IEO) as an alternate for interest_expense (DATA-INFERRED reach): 545 companies / 10,528 company-periods since 2015. Banks 318 / 6,849 periods: gain 4,497 periods at 312 banks, same as existing 2,292, conflict with InterestExpense (IE) 32 periods at 7 banks (filing-vintage differences), conflict with InterestExpenseDebt (IED) 28 periods at 6 banks. Non-banks 3,679 periods at 227 companies: gain 2,115 at 192, conflict with IE 85 at 28, with IED 205 at 24. Top-1000 cohort (1,014 ids): banks 597 periods gain at 42 banks, 1 IED conflict; non-banks 438 periods gain at 39 companies, 40 IED-conflict periods at 3 companies (AFL, CMC, TPG). Latest FY blank today for 299 of 306 banks with an FY IEO fact, and for 41 of 41 in the cohort. EFSC FY2025 gains 261,672K (FILING-CHECKED 0001025835-26-000058 R5; FY2024 282,955K matches stored).
MAIN FINDING: the existing p2 InterestExpenseDebt is a LIVE WRONG-VALUE SOURCE at banks: IED is a component inside the face total, IEO is the face "Total interest expense"; placing IEO after IED (p3) would leave these wrong. FILING-CHECKED (stored today vs face total): RBB 0001437749-25-008002 R4 FY2024 4,800K vs 117,297K; NBBK 0001104659-26-022367 R4 FY2025 5,400K vs 138,290K; MYFW 0001628280-26-012825 R5 FY2025 2,400K vs 84,419K; COLB 0000887343-25-000054 R5 FY2022 8,920K vs 78,001K; HWBK 0000893847-24-000016 R4 6 months to 2024-06-30 389K vs 18,688K. Non-bank IED corrections: CMC 0000022444-26-000041 R2 14.9M vs 105.981M; TPG 0001880661-26-000011 R5 102.3M vs 112.111M; AFL 0001628280-26-011402 R3 217M vs 220M. Non-banks file IEO with a different meaning (MTZ 0000015615-26-000020 R3 and HTZ put "Interest expense, net" on IEO; IP's face net line is InterestIncomeExpenseNet 372M and it also holds an IEO fact of 372M beside IE 551M, location not opened; 21 IEO periods at 7 non-banks are negative, 15 at SSRM, none at banks), so the rule stays BANK-GATED. IE vs IEO vintage differences at banks are reclassifications or restatements in a later filing (CIVB 0000950170-23-011581 R4 vs 0000950170-25-035961 R4; MNSB 0001437749-24-008676 R4 vs 0001437749-25-007641 R4): which vintage to show is a policy decision for the Standardization Lead and Verifier.
Proposed Stage A rows (bank group only): InterestExpense stays p1; InterestExpenseOperating p2 NEW, bank group only, excluding negative values; InterestExpenseDebt moves to p3 for banks (unchanged elsewhere). Recommendation: keep IE above IEO for the first release so no existing IE-sourced value changes; only gains and the IED corrections change. Stage B (non-bank groups) queued behind a per-group definition from the Sector Analyst. Required alongside Stage A: block interest_coverage_ratio and net_interest_income for banks (95 gained bank periods at 10 banks would newly compute interest_coverage_ratio; 55 at 6 banks would compute net_interest_income from a securities-only InvestmentIncomeInterest numerator). ENGINEERING GAP: concept_mapping and mapper/resolve.py have no group-scoped priority: needs a migration and a dry-run first.
Task 2 bdc_total_investment_income (concept 205): GrossInvestmentIncomeOperating is the only tag mapped; 3,648 canonical rows at 118 companies: BDCs (null SIC proxy) 42 / 1,361 rows; insurers SIC 63xx 47 / 1,940; SIC 6411 4 / 100; ETPs and KKR 12 / 76; banks/credit 4 / 81; other 9 / 90; no metric uses concept 205. Proposed gate: group = bdc only: 42 BDCs keep 1,361 rows, 76 non-BDCs lose 2,287 rows; in the cohort 27 of 29 companies lose the concept (ARCC, BXSL keep it); bdc_company coverage population falls from 118 to 42 (coverage_matrix.py must follow). BLOCKER: group_assignment_v2.csv assigns `bdc` to only 2 of the 42 BDCs (Sector Analyst to extend to the other 40); interim proxy null SIC + a BDC marker tag (only ARCC opened; the other 41 DATA-INFERRED). Insurers map instead (new concepts, names are the Standardization Lead's call; insurer group gate): NetInvestmentIncome face line = insurer net investment income (89 companies, 5,531 periods; 3,764 periods at 76 companies have no gross tag today, so no value now); GrossInvestmentIncomeOperating note line = insurer gross investment income (identity gross = net + InvestmentIncomeInvestmentExpense holds in 1,571 of 1,726 shared periods, 91%). Do NOT map InvestmentIncomeNet (includes realized gains at PGR); do NOT map NetInvestmentIncome for BDCs (pre-tax line; the BDC after-tax line is InvestmentIncomeOperatingAfterExpenseAndTax). Insurer evidence beyond CINF/TRV/PRU is DATA-INFERRED.
Untested: Stage B groups, IP and SSRM IEO filing locations, whether conflict-fill rows alter any counted "existing" value, the 5 other vintage banks (HTB, TFC, SMBC, BYFC, QNTO), MS FY2023 vintage (later filing confirmed; the IE 42,051K value taken from the DB, filing not opened), AXP/SOFI/SYF/CBC bank-group assignment (only AXP's face lines opened).
Next: Standardization Lead (Stage A rows, vintage ordering, insurer concept names, bank metric block); Verifier (sample 10 more gaining banks of the 312, including the 41 cohort banks); Data Engineer (group-scoped mapping capability, then dry-run the canonical_fact diff: 4,497 inserts and 28 replacements for interest_expense; -2,287 rows for the BDC gate); Sector Analyst (extend `bdc` to the other 40 BDCs; rule on AXP, SOFI, SYF, CBC).

## scrooner-7b RF-14 operating-income sample (2026-10-23): data-team/strategy/team/RF14-OPINC-SAMPLE-2026-10-23.md (uncommitted; seed 20261023; frame query and grouped backend breakdown in the file: 7 at start, 6 at end; no query over 20s)
Frame: 350 of 986 cohort companies sampled at random (query budget stopped a full scan): 19 on the derived two-input path (5.4%), 296 single-tag OperatingIncomeLoss, 35 no value. Derived path, all 19 FILING-CHECKED: 6 match a face operating line (NVDA, SNOW, RBRK, PATH, WDAY, IOT; 32%, interval about 15-54%), 13 have NO operating line on the filing (7 wrong current-year values, 6 stale old-year values), 0 presented-but-different. A guard hiding ALL derived values would hide a correct value in 6 of 19 and remove 13 wrong ones. Control: 10 of 10 single-tag values match a face line. The named holds AFG, ELS, MOG-A, MET, HONA are all single-tag (NOT derived), so a derived-path guard would not touch them; none has a face operating line (AFG and ELS store the pretax line, MET stores 6,137 which is not on the face, HONA has no 10-K). Small-sample caveat n=19. Implication for lane 3's RF-14: keep the company-scoped holds for the named single-tag cases; a derived-path rule needs a face-line test (show only when a face operating line exists in the filing), not a blanket hide. RF-13 sample still running.

## scrooner-7b RF-13 hidden-correct sample (2026-10-23): data-team/strategy/team/RF13-HIDDEN-CORRECT-SAMPLE-2026-10-23.md (uncommitted; seed 20261023; frame query, 40-row table, grouped backend breakdown in the file: pooler/app 7 at start, 6 at end; ~92 queries, none over 20s)
Frame: 66 company-periods at 66 of the 842 non-bank top-1000 companies trigger lane 3's revenue-total disagreement guard on their latest fiscal-year period (bank rule SIC 6000-6411, so REITs remain; annual only, no 10-Q quarter; the revenue tag list is the worker's assumption, so the frame could be wider with a different list). Sample: 40 of 66, all FILING-CHECKED against the income statement. Result: 7 hidden-correct (HAS, STZ, CHTR, BF-A, AR, VICR, RSG) = 17.5%, 95% interval 8.7%-32%; 33 hidden-WRONG; 0 undecidable; 8 of the 33 stored a presented subtotal rather than the labelled total. By stored tag: stored picked the 606 tag (RevenueFromContractWithCustomer...) in 38 rows (5 correct, 33 wrong) and Revenues in 2 (both correct). So on the latest-FY frame the guard mostly hides WRONG values. This differs from the doc 37 sample (15 of 22 correct), which used a different frame: do not pool them. Caveats: n=40, annual only. Implication: the dominant defect is the component-beats-total mapping (606 tag wins over Revenues when both are filed, as at GE); hiding is the safe interim, but the cohort fix is the structural pick (NA-9: choose the labelled total line), which would recover values instead of leaving blanks. Extrapolation to all 66 is DATA-INFERRED (about 54 wrong current-year revenues; interval wide).

## Root fix #1 built: total debt from balance-sheet carrying lines (lane 3, 2026-10-24): branch work/data-engineer/lane3-total-debt, c405a05 on ab4bed0 (pushed; NOT on main; flag OFF)
Full unit suite 1557 passed, 21 skipped; mypy and ruff clean; every mutant killed (a few survived first until tests were added for lease-family pairing, the company-stated-total check and the DebtCurrent threshold). Mechanism (common/debt_carrying.py, pure; guard r8_carrying_lines in evaluate_total_debt): total debt = noncurrent + current block [+ short-term borrowings]; tags LongTermDebtNoncurrent, LongTermDebtCurrent or DebtCurrent, ShortTermBorrowings or CommercialPaper; runs ahead of the legacy paths so a face-value or rounded LongTermDebt never sets the total; lease-inclusive noncurrent pairs only with the lease-inclusive current line; short-term dropped if equal to current LTD; does NOT apply when a line is missing, when LongTermDebt differs from noncurrent + current by > 10%, or when the company's stated total (DebtLongtermAndShorttermCombinedAmount) differs from the carrying total by > 10%; a filed DebtCurrent that overlaps its filed parts and matches no subset sum is held; DebtCurrent at or above the sum of the listed parts (TSLA-shaped) is accepted. Existing guards r1, r5, r6, r7 still run behind it; debt.total_debt_consistency@1 enables all including r8; evaluate_total_debt's default stays r1-r7.
Targets from stored tags: AAPL Q3 FY26 84,344 (stored 84,297); GOOG FY2025 48,543 (stored 49,085); AMZN FY2025 68,851 (stored 69,291); AMZN Q2 2026 132,549 (stored 133,320); PFE FY2020 39,836; DPZ FY2024 4,975,338K; MU FY2026 5,179 (stored 2,792; matches the pilot's filing-checked figure). NOT reachable from tags: CVX FY2016 (printed 46,033: short-term debt 10,840 already contains the 6,253 current maturities and DebtCurrent 19,840 is not a subset sum; held with a visible reason; needs face-line/presentation evidence) and AAPL FY2015 (printed 64,462: the 53,463 noncurrent line exists only as a non-authoritative conflict copy; hard rule forbids using it, r7 keeps it blank; needs the conflict-resolved value).
Certify gate: debt_not_carrying_lines (I8, WRONG_VALUE) compares stored total to the carrying lines, exact to about a dollar; debt_carrying_lines_undecidable is REVIEW; the loader reads authoritative carrying tags into carrying_facts; tests reproduce the AAPL, GOOG and AMZN pilot errors as gating wrong values (the suite raised none before).
Dry run (read-only, one connection, 20s, 10 backends) on the 10 pilot companies with r1-r8 ON vs stored: 167 instants changed; 8 of 10 companies touched (NVDA, META unchanged); 9 value-to-blank (7 TSLA 2011-2018 [two were r5 floor holds that pre-existed], 1 LLY 2014-03-31, 1 AAPL FY2015); 0 blank-to-value; median relative change 0.59%; about 20 instants change by > 10% (mostly MU, one TSLA). Before/after file debt_carrying_cohort.jsonl in the scratchpad, not committed. Not done: population dry-run; Verifier sample; release (flag off; needs the usual dry-run review and Verifier sample).

## Verifier sample of root fix #1 (total debt from carrying lines), 2026-10-24: RELEASE GATE FAILS, 3 of 37 new values wrong (all TSLA 2017); data-team/strategy/team/VERIFY-TOTAL-DEBT-CARRYING-2026-10-24.md (uncommitted); no DB query, before/after file + SEC filings only
Sample (all 46 FILING-CHECKED with accession and R page in the report): frame = the 167 changed instants in debt_carrying_cohort.jsonl (branch work/data-engineer/lane3-total-debt @c405a05). The required cells came to 36: all 20 MU, all 10 TSLA, all 9 value-to-blank (7 are TSLA), plus AAPL Q3 FY26, GOOG FY2025, AMZN FY2025 and Q2 2026; plus 10 random from the other 131 (seed random.Random('20261024-totaldebt')). Where AMZN and GOOG bury the current portion of debt inside accrued liabilities the filing's own facts were used via SEC companyconcept.
Results: MU 20/20 new right (old 20 wrong); named AAPL 84,344, GOOG 48,543, AMZN 68,851 and 132,549 4/4 right (old 4 wrong); random 9/10 right with old 10 wrong (the tenth, GOOG 2023-12-31, is a definition question: the printed long-term debt line 13,253M includes 1,383M of finance leases, the new 12,870M is notes only, consistent with GOOG FY2025); TSLA 2017 0/3 right (new value wrong; old wrong too). Total 37: new right 33, new wrong 3, old stored wrong 37 of 37.
Mechanism of the 3 TSLA errors (one cause): the rule adds the debt-note DebtCurrent (debt only, no capital leases) to the face line "Long-term debt and capital leases" (leases included) and drops the separate noncurrent line for related-party convertible notes. 2017-06-30 new 7,883.1M vs face 7,941.8M (0001564590-17-015705 R2); 2017-09-30 9,957.1M vs 10,008.3M (0001564590-17-021343 R2); 2017-03-31 8,112.2M vs at least 7,987.9M (one current row not rendered on the R page). The path that lets them through: "DebtCurrent at or above the parts is accepted".
Value-to-blank (9): 3 hide an already-correct value (LLY 2014-03-31: 9.1 + 5,255.3 = 5,264.4, 0000059478-14-000102 R4, the hold fires wrongly; TSLA 2011 and 2012: borrowings-only totals matching the face, both before 2015, outside the display window); 6 replace a wrong value with a blank where the face gives the right one: AAPL FY2015 should show 64,462M; TSLA 2014-06 and 2014-09 about 2.36-2.38B; TSLA 2018-03, 2018-06, 2018-09 10.76B, 11.62B, 11.78B (blank is safer than the old values, but each total can be read off the face).
NEXT: (1) lane 3: remove the "DebtCurrent at or above the parts" path or require current and noncurrent lines to share a lease perimeter and include every noncurrent debt face line; re-test TSLA 2017; (2) fix the false hold at LLY 2014-03-31; (3) lease-perimeter decision from the Standardization Lead: MU and TSLA are lease-inclusive, GOOG and AMZN borrowings-only; then a fresh sample with a lease-perimeter stratum. ## Standardization Lead decision cards (2026-10-24): PROPOSALS, adopted by Dhairya under delegation as below (reversible); nothing verified by the Verifier yet; cards untracked in the shared tree
CARD A: data-team/strategy/team/DC-TOTAL-DEBT-LEASE-PERIMETER-2026-10-24.md. D1 total debt = carrying amount of borrowings + finance (capital) lease liabilities, current + noncurrent; operating leases NEVER; built from two stored parts (borrowings; finance leases), each with the lines and filing it came from, so AC-1 (finance leases in debt, unanswered) can choose either perimeter without a rebuild; proposed AC-1 option (b) include. D2 a lease-inclusive line plus a separate lease disclosure: show "Total debt (includes finance leases)" with both parts visible; borrowings-only only where the filing's own arithmetic proves combined = borrowings + leases (AC-1 sub-question C2); NEVER sum a lease-inclusive line with a debt-only line (root cause of the 3 wrong TSLA 2017 values: remove the "DebtCurrent at or above the parts is accepted" path); the filer's own ASC 842 lease-location tags (FinanceLeaseLiability{Current,Noncurrent}StatementOfFinancialPositionExtensibleList) say which line holds its finance leases: a deterministic guard against double counting. D3 related-party notes count as debt only when the filer's debt note counts them (TSLA R60), never by the generic DueToRelatedParties* tag alone; convertible notes carried in temporary equity are equity, not debt; a current line labelled as including current maturities is the whole current side (pair it only with the noncurrent line: LLY 2014 should give 5,264.4M with no overlap hold); never use a pre-refinancing DebtCurrent subtotal (CVX; NA-6). D4 ten metrics consume total debt (graph_def.py lines 42-105): debt_to_equity, net_cash, net_cash_per_share, roic, ev_sales, ev_ebitda, net_debt_ebitda, debt_to_ebitda, piotroski_f_score, zero_debt; including finance leases must ship with AC-6 (lease amortization in D&A), AC-15 (lease interest) and AC-5/AC-20 (FCF). D5 accountant cards AC-1 (with AC-5/AC-6, AC-15), AC-2, AC-3, AC-4 already cover it. FILING-CHECKED by the Lead: MU 2025-08-28 borrowings 11,533M / incl. finance leases 14,577M (its "Current debt" 560M is entirely finance leases); TSLA 2017-06-30 7,515,847K (R60) / 7,941,839K (capital leases about 425,992K by difference), 2017-09-30 incl. leases 10,008,321K; GOOG 2023-12-31 12,870M / 14,616M (the note's "Total debt" 14,746M is before a 130M discount and was the stored value); GOOG FY2025 48,543M / 51,043M; AMZN FY2025 68,851M / 81,137M (its 87,339M "lease liabilities" line includes operating leases); LLY 2014-03-31 5,264.4M; CVX FY2016 46,019M / 46,126M. Not tested: LLY's and TSLA 2017-09-30's lease amounts, how many filers file the lease-location tags, whether each company's D&A includes finance-lease amortization.
DHAIRYA DECISION (release call for D4): until the accountant answers AC-1, the TEN consuming metrics are HIDDEN with the visible reason "definition pending accountant review" (option ii), while the statement row shows the corrected two-part build with its perimeter label. Reason: all 37 of 37 stored cells the Verifier checked were wrong, and the plan keeps accountant-gated definitions hidden-with-reason until a reviewer is named; this lowers coverage for those metrics until the founder names the accountant (cost accepted). The build (two parts, perimeter as a named parameter) proceeds now so AC-1 needs no rebuild.
CARD B: data-team/strategy/team/DC-INTEREST-EXPENSE-AND-INVESTMENT-INCOME-2026-10-22.md. (1) Bank interest expense: InterestExpense and InterestExpenseOperating are one exact pair at banks (two tag eras of the same face line); InterestExpenseDebt should be REMOVED for banks, not kept as a third fallback (opened the five cases: debt-note or narrative figure at RBB 4,800K, NBBK 5.4M, MYFW 2.4M; the face "Interest on borrowings" line at COLB; at HWBK it is the face NON-interest expense row "Loan expense", 389K). ## Lane 3 total-debt V3 follow-up (2026-10-24): branch work/data-engineer/lane3-total-debt, head 6ac6f59 (prior V3 core commit e791a1b; pushed; flag OFF; dry-run only; no DB writes)
Changes: (1) short-term slot includes OtherShortTermBorrowings; same-amount tags count as one line (CommercialPaper + OtherShortTermBorrowings do not double count); a container (largest amount equals the sum of the others) is counted once; distinct amounts held unless a stated total proves the sum. (2) refine_copies: the most precise copy of a line wins when the other is a rounding variant (half-unit tolerance, capped at 1e6), applied only under r8, in both the loader and the certify loader; a lone non-authoritative copy is never used. (3) Labels: a zero lease total derived from face lines is labelled finance_leases_not_determined (not a filed zero); when only one lease half is authoritative: finance_leases_incomplete. ## Root fix #2 built: split re-base pass (Period Engineer, 2026-10-24): COMPLETE for this scope; branch work/period-engineer/split-rebase, commit e312dfb (one commit on origin/work/period-engineer/migrations-0098-0099 cb2c688; pushed to the branch only; worktree /Users/vikash/wt-split-rebase); flag split.event_basis@1 OFF; nothing applied
Code: corporate_actions/share_basis.py. Events from three kinds of filing evidence (split-ratio facts in instant and duration contexts; filed pairs = same tag and period in two consecutive periodic filings differing by one clean ratio, 3:2 included; share-count steps); an event is identified by its ratio and the observed basis boundary, never by a day window; confirmed only with two kinds; a single-kind candidate is `review`: never re-bases, hides the cells it touches; candidates contradicted by ratio-1 filed pairs, or that are the product of two confirmed splits (TSLA 5x3), are rejected. Factor set per filing from its source filing date (basis_date > filing_date). Company invariant: the same unexplained basis ratio on 2 or more periods between one pair of filings hides every per-share and share cell of that company (no company tripped it). Every periodic, non-derived copy in the right unit is converted by its own filing's factor; the latest copy is shown when copies agree, otherwise the cell is hidden with a reason; share counts multiplied by the factor. Writes (mode `on` only) via the shared archive-then-replace helper with restore_run for rollback. Mode `off` issues no query. One line changed in the unified rule-flags module (rule registered with a dry-run path). Brought in unchanged: common/rule_flags.py, its test and pipeline-recompute.yml from lane1-rule-flags-v2 771cf6e; mapper/canonical_archive.py, common/derivation_policy.py and tests from held/ru1-per-share 91c6171. Dry-run script scripts/split_rebase_dryrun.py (session read-only, one connection, 20s, refuses above 12 pooler/app backends; started at 5-6); per-cell tables for the Verifier: doc/data-moat/team/backups/split_rebase_dryrun_cohort11_20261024.csv.gz and ..._with_ru1_20261024.csv.gz in the worktree.
