## Data Engineer (lane 1) answer to the founder: "if everything is in the filing, why are we avoiding reading it?" (2026-10-24)
Why companyfacts: cheapest route to 5,200 companies (one JSON per company, about 5,200 requests, no parsing, values keyed by tag and period). What it throws away (first four FILING-CHECKED in our cases, last DATA-INFERRED): dimensions (segments, share classes, legal entities stripped); the filing's own arrangement (which line is on the face of which statement, the calculation tree); provenance and copies (all copies of a period across filings flattened; conflicts only visible later); cover vs balance-sheet dates (kept apart only by our later anchoring work); custom tags (companyfacts and Frames carry no company extension tags: TSLA 2,634K, GOOG lease facts and Entergy's subsidiaries live only in the filing); attribution errors (SEC served four groups' Q2 10-Qs under a subsidiary's CIK).
Simpler or gone if the filing is the source: the authority gate and conflict resolution (each period read from its own filing), P1's header/dei guesswork (plain parsing), the share-date anchor (cover and balance-sheet facts separate by construction), the lease perimeter and the structural revenue pick (read from the statement). STILL NEEDED: mapping tags to standard concepts and the equivalence work; the restatement choice (which vintage a value belongs to); TTM and Q4 derivation; split adjustment; template and applicability.
Effort/risk: about 40,000 instances at 1-20 MB each (a few hundred GB raw) -> use SEC Financial Statement Data Sets instead: 2-3 files per quarter, about 60-130 MB each, with num and pre, about 45 quarterly downloads and a few hundred requests; they carry dimensions and presentation but not custom-tag semantics; disk has about 9 GiB free so stream from a runner and keep only the cohort rows in Postgres (feasible if the pilot says the rows fit). Estimate 3-4 engineer-weeks for a cohort reader + reconciliation, 6-8 with Verifier sampling (ASSUMED, +/-25%). Risks: bad XBRL in filings; the reader shares the same blind spots as our other parsers; mapping must be built either way.
RECOMMENDATION: yes, make the filing the primary route for the top-1000 cohort, in stages, NOT as a replacement yet. First measurable step (about 4 days): load one quarter of the data sets (2025q4) for the 986 cohort companies, compare headline cells for 10 golden companies with our stored values, list every disagreement with its accession: a number for how many cells the filing route fixes and how many it breaks. Then a Verifier sample, then the decision to expand. (The running as-filed pilot, branch work/data-engineer/as-filed-pilot, covers the same ground for the 10 pilot companies across 2015+; do not start a duplicate.)

## As-filed pilot RESULT (Data Engineer, 2026-10-24): COMPLETE; branch work/data-engineer/as-filed-pilot, commit 2487435 (pushed; code and small CSV tables in pipeline/scripts/as_filed_pilot/; report AS-FILED-PILOT-2026-10-24.md in the worktree .../scratchpad/wt-asfiled/data-team/strategy/team/, not the main tree); no DB writes, no migration; DB reads one connection read-only; raw extractions 3.3 MB gz uncommitted in the scratchpad. Route: SEC Financial Statement Data Sets quarterly zips (sub, num, pre): 47 requests for 2015q1-2026q3 vs about 1,800 for instance + linkbases. Run: 461 filings parsed (346 10-Q, 115 10-K), 98,711 non-dimensioned face facts (balance sheet, income statement, cash flow); 68 SEC requests (47 FSDS quarters, 1 test download, 4 extra quarters for ABBV and CVX, 16 for the calculation linkbases = 8 filings x index + _cal.xml); 4.41 GB downloaded, about 27 min wall, 4 req/s through the shared limiter, one process, one zip at a time deleted after use, free disk never below 6.9 GB. 467 filings in core.filing since 2015-01-01; 453 matched; of the 14 not in FSDS: 9 amendments without statements, 4 filed after the newest FSDS quarter (DATA-INFERRED), 1 real miss (AVGO 10-Q for the period ending 2026-02-01, cause unchecked); 8 FSDS filings absent from core.filing are the predecessor registrants Google Inc (CIK 1288776) and Broadcom Ltd (CIK 1441634) (our store holds that data only as comparatives in later filings, DATA-INFERRED).
Versus stored: face facts reproduced exactly by the stored companyfacts value 92,215 of 98,711 (93.4%); non-custom face facts missing from stored: 0; custom extension tags absent from stored by design 6,310 (6.4%); value differs 186 (185 = CommonStockParOrStatedValuePerShare: FSDS truncates to 4 decimals, so par 0.00001 reads 0.0000). The face route reproduces only 92,215 of 192,055 stored us-gaap facts (48%) in those filings: the rest are NOTE facts which FSDS does not carry. Headline cells (tiny presentation rule, not the standardization layer): revenue (presentation total line) 714 agree / 4 disagree (LLY FY2016 + 3 quarters: stored 18,312.8M, every 10-K prints 21,222.1M; stored carries the Elanco recast basis, DATA-INFERRED link to ledger row 70); total assets 438/0; CFO 476/0; net income 744/1 (rule artifact: picked ProfitLoss at TSLA); diluted EPS 503/204: ALL 204 are SPLIT BASIS, not extraction (stored = as-filed / a split ratio; in at least 64 the ratio is WRONG, DATA-INFERRED from ratios: GOOG pre-2022 divided by 400 = the 20:1 split applied twice; NVDA pre-2021 by 16 = 4:1 applied twice with no 10:1). The face "Total" revenue line picked the right tag in every filing (the total tag varies by filer and year: Revenues, SalesRevenueNet, or the 606 tag).
Known wrong cells (16, incl. ABBV, CVX and GOOG-composition additions): filing-only (FSDS face lines) fixed 10 / partial 3 / not fixed 3; HYBRID (calc-linkbase slot + API value) fixed 13 / partial 0 / not fixed 3. Filing-only fixed: AAPL 2020-03-28 and 2020-06-27 commercial paper, TSLA 2018-09-30, NVDA FY2015, MU 2021-09-02, ABBV 2022-03-31 and 2017-12-31, CVX 2015-03-31 and 2014-09-30, and revenue total vs 606; partial: GOOG 2015-09, 2016-03, 2016-06 (total right, commercial paper vs current capital lease composition is note-level and missing); not fixed: GOOG 2023-12-31 finance leases (note facts) and the two EPS cells. Hybrid fixes everything filing-only fixed EXCEPT TSLA, plus GOOG 2023-12-31 (perimeter from the debt-note calc role DebtInstrumentCarryingAmount + FinanceLeaseLiability, API holds 363 and 1,746) and the three GOOG 2015-16 cells (API holds CapitalLeaseObligationsCurrent 238/223/220M). Hybrid not fixed: TSLA 2,634K (custom tag, absent from the API) and the two EPS cells (AAPL FY2018 11.91, GOOG Q2 2022 1.21: both routes read the as-filed value correctly, the API raw value is also correct; the error is the split-adjusted _resolved row, which neither route fixes without a split table; the filing date tells you GOOG 2022 Q2 needs no adjustment). The calc linkbase confirms the missed lines are children of the face total: AAPL OtherShortTermBorrowings and ABBV ShortTermBorrowings under LiabilitiesCurrent; CVX CapitalLeaseObligationsNoncurrent and TSLA's related-party convertible under Liabilities; NVDA LongTermDebt is a child, the rounded ConvertibleDebtNoncurrent is not; GOOG DebtCurrent is a leaf in the calc tree (composition only in the notes). Stored-correct cells the extractor would break: none at value level apart from the 185 par-value truncations (0.19% of face facts); going filing-only would drop the 52% of stored us-gaap facts that are note facts and 8,978 face-tag facts that sit in other columns. Total-line flag: FSDS label heuristic precision 96/96, recall 73% against the filers' own calc parents on 6 filings (misses are note-tree parents, not face totals).
STILL NEEDS THE STANDARDIZATION LAYER: tag-to-concept mapping (NetIncomeLoss vs ProfitLoss); lease perimeter policy; restatement vintage choice (a period appears in up to 6 filings; stored matches the latest in some cells and the original in others); split basis and TTM/Q4/YTD derivation (not touched); custom tags (filing read needed because the API drops them), note-level facts (from the API), predecessor registrants. FSDS LIMITS (these decide filing-only vs hybrid): ddate is rounded to the nearest month-end (MU 2021-09-02 reads 2021-08-31) and there is no start date, only qtrs (without applying the same rounding to our dates the first match showed 11,001 false "absent" facts, almost all MU and AVGO); values have 4 decimals and per-share facts have uom USD; no calculation linkbase in FSDS (read _cal.xml from the filing folder, 2 requests per filing); pre.txt must be sorted by (report, line).
RECOMMENDATION (pilot agent): the data supports the founder's HYBRID. Keep the API as the value feed (93.4% exact, no non-custom face fact missing). Use the filing for the slot record only (face order, the total line, the calc tree). Add a filing read for custom tags and exact dates. NEXT cheap step: build the slot record for debt, D&A and capex on the 30-company set from _cal.xml and replay the 16 cells as a regression set. Untested: the extractor on the 30-company set or the full population; calc linkbases read for 8 filings only; no production fallback or write path; why FSDS lacks the AVGO 2026-03 10-Q; LLY's 8-K recast link and the "filed after the newest FSDS quarter" explanation are DATA-INFERRED.

## FOUNDER DESIGN STATEMENT (2026-10-24, his words): "api as data source, and with the help of filing we should create company-wise mapper, because mostly they use the same format every time; we should have a gate: when they change format, then we fetch the full filing and adjust/extend the mapper for the company"
Dhairya's reading (agreed with refinements, consistent with the as-filed pilot result): (1) API = values for standard tags (93.4% exact on face facts, 0 non-custom face facts missing; note facts only via the API); the full filing = everything (custom tags 6.4% of face facts, dimensions, presentation/calculation/label linkbases, text); (2) the company-wise mapper is a table of per-company SLOT RECORDS per statement line (which tags make up the total in THIS company's filing), versioned by effective period and evidence accession, built from the 2015+ filings (FSDS pre/num for structure; _cal.xml only where needed); (3) the format-change GATE runs on every new 10-K/10-Q: compare the filing's face-statement fingerprint (lines, tags, order, total lines, calc parents) with the company's current slot record; identical -> map automatically from the API; different -> the affected cells are HELD with the visible reason "format changed, under review", the full filing is fetched, a mapper update is PROPOSED, checked (Verifier or human where a definition is involved), and only then released; (4) not solved by the mapper: restatement vintage, split basis, TTM/Q4 derivation, lease/accounting policy (accountant), custom-tag values (filing read), predecessor registrants. Known real format changes the gate must catch: bank InterestExpense -> InterestExpenseOperating from 2021-09; CECL net loans/allowance; ASC 842 lease lines; ASC 606 revenue; acquisitions/new lines; fiscal-year-end changes.

## Derivation catalog (Standardization Lead, 2026-10-24): COMPLETE (proposal only); data-team/strategy/team/DERIVATION-CATALOG-2026-10-24.md (873 words; uncommitted); ledger +3 rows, tag inbox +5 lines, 3 mind inbox entries
HEADLINE: a derivation ALREADY RUNNING in the pipeline writes WRONG values: mapper/concept_fallback.py::resolve_balance_sheet_identity_fallback (commit f0215f8). Bug 1 (sign): the SQL computes a.value + (other_sign * o.value) + coalesce(adj.total_adj, 0) = assets - equity + NCI/temporary equity, but the docstring says the adjustment is subtracted. Bug 2 (double count): stockholders_equity priority 2 is the NCI-inclusive tag; when equity comes from it, NCI is counted a second time. WMT 2026-07-31 (FILING-CHECKED 0000104169-26-000154 R4): stored liabilities 202,237M; filing 293,914 - 104,506 - 293 = 189,115M. AT&T 2026-06-30 (FILING-CHECKED 0000732717-26-000297 R5): stored 319,920M; filing 299,920M (AT&T's face has no parent-equity line, only the NCI-inclusive total). VLO (ledger row 111) comes from this same code path, untraced until now. Reach (DATA-INFERRED): 5,252 cohort total_liabilities_resolved cells since 2015 at 185 companies carry a nonzero adjustment, so all are wrong; the latest cell is wrong at 110 companies; 54 stockholders_equity_resolved cells go through the same path. Proposed fix: liabilities = LiabilitiesAndStockholdersEquity - total equity including NCI - temporary equity, recording which equity tag was the input. (Only WMT, T and VLO via the ledger are FILING-CHECKED; most of the 5,252 are not.)
Other proofs (FILING-CHECKED): Q4 = FY - 9M works when inputs are on the same basis (AAPL Q4 FY25 revenue 416,161 - 313,695 = 102,466 = the earnings release, 0000320193-25-000077 Ex 99.1); Q4 needs the 9M copy that matches the FY's basis (NRG FY2018: the 10-K quarterly note 0001013871-19-000007 R149 is already on the recast basis; Q1-Q3 revenue 7,486 and OI 933 = the 2019 10-Q comparative 0001013871-19-000035 R2 = 9M pretax 572; the original Q3 10-Q 0001013871-18-000039 R2 shows 620; Q4 pretax = 467 - 572 = -105; pairing the earliest-filed copies gives -153, wrong); working-capital changes CANNOT come from balance-sheet differences (WMT AP change in the cash flow 1,611 vs balance-sheet change 4,395, 0000104169-26-000055 R8; AAPL AR 6,682 vs 6,367, 0000320193-25-000079 R8); summing face lines works for D&A (TXN = Depreciation 1,918 + CapitalizedComputerSoftwareAmortization 81, 0000097476-26-000059 R7); MSFT D&A cannot be derived (custom tag msft_DepreciationAmortizationAndOther, 0001193125-26-323660 R6); the cash identity holds only against restricted-cash-inclusive cash (WMT FY26 41,565 - 26,350 - 13,553 + 123 = 1,785 = the change in restricted-cash-inclusive cash 11,321 - 9,536, not the balance-sheet cash line 10,727).
Sizing (DATA-INFERRED): deriving fills little of the latest-period residue: up to about 85 D&A cells by summing face lines; 0 cells among AP/AR changes, collaborative revenue, DPS/dividends, interest expense, shares and the 7,724 pending closures (state closures, not missing values). Why blank: cf_ap_change 262 of 358 file the combined AP + accrued tag; other_income_expense_net 202 of 275 file OtherNonoperatingIncomeExpense (vocabulary gap; needs a new definition card, called AC-22 by the Lead [COLLIDES with the growth card AC-22: renumber to AC-34]; priority 2 currently maps an interest tag); interest_expense about 45 vocabulary gaps (InterestExpenseNonoperating, InterestAndDebtExpense) and about 68 file only a net line. HISTORY is the big lever: the cohort period_gap has 10,173 additive Q4/quarter cells; a seeded sample of 60 found 40 blocked by conflicting copies, 11 with no YTD fact, 9 clean (matches the 2026-10-17 census).
Build order proposed: (1) fix the balance-identity code (a correctness fix for 5,252 values); (2) pair Q4/quarter inputs on the same basis, with a Verifier sample (about 6,800 history cells); (3) sum face lines using the as-filed pilot's per-company line record (D&A, capex, debt), then read share classes from the filing instance; (4) TTM and composite metrics recompute. Stamp rule for a derived value: state `derived`, rule_id@version, input fact ids with accession, tag, period and basis, and the arithmetic, written to core.derived_fact_input (0103) and cell_evidence; a derived value never feeds another subtraction. Not tested: most of the 5,252 cells (3 companies filing-checked); the 9 "clean" sampled gaps classified only roughly; the D&A summation for the about 85 cells (only TXN checked); AR "receivables" lines, the SM continuing-ops tag mix and the zero-debt claims. Files in the scratchpad: ident_rows.tsv (the 7,548 identity rows carrying an adjustment, for the dry-run), q4_sample.tsv (the 60-gap sample). NEXT: the Data Engineer owner of concept_fallback.py corrects the sign and the equity input behind a flag with a dry-run; the Verifier samples about 30 of the 5,252 cells; the Period Engineer takes the same-basis Q4 pairing rule as an RF-04 v2 candidate. Decision for the founder (not taken): this is a LIVE wrong-value bug, small and mechanical: recommend making it the first shipped fix once he says go.

## EDGAR atlas + Scrooner v2 design (Head of Data Product agent, 2026-10-24): COMPLETE (research/design only; no DB writes, no code, no commits); files (uncommitted): data-team/strategy/team/EDGAR-ATLAS-2026-10-24.md (about 1,420 words; sections A-D + corrections table; real example MU FY2026 10-K 0000723125-26-000023 and its earnings 8-K 0000723125-26-000018) and data-team/strategy/team/SCROONER-V2-DESIGN-PROPOSAL-2026-10-24.md (about 998 words; opens with the founder decision sheet; sources, storage, slot-record table, format-change gate, proof stamps, serving, certificate, same/changes/stop, build order with metrics); 6 findings in mind/inbox/20261024-1252* and -125300*. SEC access about 40 requests (4 req/s, one process); 5.2 MB downloaded and deleted; free disk stayed above 6.9 GB; about 12 light read-only queries on one connection (20s, 9-10 backends)
NEW FINDINGS (correct earlier docs): (1) Schedule 13D/13G collection BROKE in 2025: since 2024-12-18 they are filed as form type "SCHEDULE 13G/13D" with an XML document including percent of class and shares (MU 0002100119-26-000795, Vanguard 7.47%); beneficial_ownership.py reads only the old "SC 13D/G" types; rows per quarter fell from 1,452-12,818 in 2024 to 2-19 in 2025-26; this also starves the CUSIP crosswalk that 13F and N-PORT depend on. (2) The Financial Statement AND NOTES data sets fix the pilot's main limits (field guide read, no zip pulled): they carry note values, dimensions, all text facts and each filer's calculation tree; their number table has datp/durp/dcml, so exact dates, durations and decimals can be recovered; the "rounded dates, no start date, no calc tree" limits apply to the plain statement data sets only; doc 22 called them "redundant" and doc 31 called the notes data sets "out of scope": both now wrong. (3) companyfacts carries only numeric, undimensioned, standard facts: MU's FY2026 10-K has 1,421 facts (682 dimensional, 137 text, 77 custom); we hold 519 rows for that filing; only 5 dei tags exist in core.concept, so the auditor name, the error-correction (restatement) flag, the cybersecurity flags and the 10b5-1 arrangement text never reach us. (4) SEC publishes all Forms 3/4/5 as one quarterly bulk file (2026q3: 8.7 MB, 38,153 submissions, with the 10b5-1 column): can replace roughly 38,000 per-filing Form 4 fetches a quarter. (5) Financial_Report.xlsx is no longer generated (404 for AAPL FY2025 and both MU 10-Ks checked; exists for AAPL FY2019). (6) Discrete Q4 diluted EPS is filed only in the 8-K Ex-99.1 earnings release (the 10-K tags only the full year: MU Q4 32.87 vs FY 74.33). (7) Data already sitting UNUSED in core.fact (DB counts, active companies): pay versus performance 1,226-1,791; debt maturities 2,461; operating-lease payments 3,938; cash taxes paid 3,748; backlog 1,032; remaining buyback authorization 1,135. (8) 8-K signals since 2025 stored but never turned into flags: non-reliance 4.02 at 164 companies, auditor change 4.01 at 843, delisting notice 3.01 at 842. (9) NT 10-K/10-Q late-filing notices and Form 25 are not in core.filing.
THREE FOUNDER DECISIONS (agent's recommendations): (1) hybrid as the primary route for the top-1000 with visible "format changed, under review" holds: YES (pilot: hybrid fixed 13 of 16 known-wrong cells vs 10 for filing-only); (2) may furnished, unaudited 8-K Ex-99.1 earnings-release figures be shown? recommend Q4 only, labelled unaudited and tied to full year minus nine months; company guidance waits (doc 02 keeps forecasts out); (3) data budget: load the notes data sets for the cohort (about 0.3 GB in a peak month, streamed, only cohort rows kept) and stop growing core.fact with unmapped note facts: yes, but only after a one-month size pilot. COULD NOT VERIFY: what is inside a notes-data-set zip (field guide only; not checked that the text table holds every dei flag); when SEC stopped generating Financial_Report.xlsx; how fast full-text search indexes new filings and how stable it is (undocumented API); whether our nightly uses the per-filer API or the bulk zip; how many filers use Ex-13; why a few 13D/G rows still arrive in 2025-26 (assumed amendments under the old form type); how often company formats change year over year (assumed low, as the founder said); how reliably earnings-release tables parse across filers; effort estimates (none given).

## Open resources survey (Tag Expert, 2026-10-24): COMPLETE (research only; no DB writes, no code, no commits); data-team/strategy/team/OPEN-SOURCES-FOR-COVERAGE-2026-10-24.md (new, uncommitted; seven sources ranked by coverage gained over effort); every downloaded file deleted
MAIN FINDING (FILING-CHECKED): the 2024 US-GAAP taxonomy added a new tag, InterestExpenseNonoperating, and non-banks now use it for their plain "Interest expense" line; our interest_expense concept does not map it, so these series STOP once a company switches: Colgate's 10-Q 0000021665-25-000053 (R2, Q3 2025, 67M) and Avery Dennison's 10-Q 0000008818-25-000049 (R4, 33.3M) both use the new tag; our interest_expense and interest_expense_resolved stop at FY2023 for Colgate and Q1 2024 for Avery. The tag is absent from the 2015, 2019, 2022 and 2023 FASB schemas and present from 2024. Population (DATA-INFERRED, from company_sec_tag, not limited to the operating universe): 1,542 companies have recent facts on the new tag and no recent InterestExpense or InterestExpenseDebt. In FSDS 2025q4, 1,469 of the 1,898 income-statement rows on this tag read plain "Interest expense"; 217 read "Interest expense, net" (Rollins R4), so any mapping needs a LABEL CHECK.
CORRECTIONS (two, the Tag Expert's own): interest.md said the tag exists "from the 2018-era taxonomy": it first appears in 2024. The bank verdict "InterestExpenseOperating from 2021-09" took a COMPARATIVE period for the switch date: that tag also exists only from 2024 (EFSC first filed it on 2025-08-01). Cause: first_period_end in the tag library was read as the adoption date; ERA RULES MUST BE KEYED TO THE FILING'S TAXONOMY VERSION, NOT TO PERIOD DATES. (Dhairya: I repeated the 2021-09 date to the founder and in PLAN.md and in the instructions to lane 3 / the Standardization Lead's bank card: those statements are superseded; re-check the Stage A bank rows and the vintage ordering decision against the corrected era, and the CECL-era claims in bank-loans-and-allowance.md, not re-checked.)
Other: the taxonomy's essence-alias and general-special links are NOT a tag-equivalence source (22 essence-alias links, all on deprecated items; no general-special links); equivalence and perimeter come from the calculation tree (6,633 links), the newer meta-model links and the deprecation links (405, with replacements). Residue test (FSDS 2025q4 face statements, per company without the _resolved twins): collaborative_arrangement_revenue 708: 647 have no face line, 30 have collaboration facts only by dimension, 26 companies ever file the tag = EXPLAINED (applicability, not a gap); cf_ap_change 358: 244 file the combined "payables and accrued" line, which the FASB tree puts above AP = explained, never fill as AP; cf_ar_change 329: 174 file a broader receivables line, 16 insurers file premiums receivable = perimeter decision; other_income_expense_net 275: 185 file OtherNonoperatingIncomeExpense (a component of the mapped total) = fills only if the concept is defined as that line; amortization_of_intangibles 149: 109 show only a D&A total = explained as combined; dividends_paid 149: 11 on PaymentsOfOrdinaryDividends, 30 show only minority/preferred payouts, about 96 have no line in face or notes = fill 11, the rest supports "no dividend"; dividends per share 163: about 99 have no line in face or notes; TSN, ERIE, SBAC, RKT, VNOM report per share class only = mostly non-payers, class rule needed for those five; interest_expense 128: 21 on the new tag, 14 on InterestAndDebtExpense, 34 show only net interest lines = fill 21, plus 14 with a perimeter flag; D&A 126: 66 show separate depreciation and amortization lines, 29 banks/insurers use the accretion-inclusive line = fill 66 by summing (NUE R6: 910 + 191), 29 with a flag; total_debt 79: 54 show no face debt line, AES and TXT report debt only by member = mostly explained. The Notes data set added almost nothing for these residue families; its useful part is each filer's calculation tree (ODFL's puts interest inside its non-operating total, OXY's puts it inside a custom cost total). edgartools agreed on 1 of 5 tested mappings this run (3 of 12 cumulative); it labels the combined AP line "Change in Accounts Payable" at confidence 1.0.
Evidence/commands: FASB 2026 zip (7.4 MB) and the 2015/2019/2022/2023/2024 schemas and 2023/2024/2025 deprecation files from xbrl.fasb.org; FSDS 2025q4 from a peer's existing copy (read only); FSN 2025_11 (292 MB) read straight from the zip, then deleted; 13 R-files opened (LNG, SWKS, ROL, NUE, PFE, TXN, PKG, XEL, CL, AVY, ODFL, OXY, JPM) all matched FSDS on tag and label; 1 companyconcept call (EFSC), 1 full-text search (finds Apple's Q4 release 0000320193-25-000077 Ex. 99.1), 5 HEAD/size checks; SEC traffic <= 1 req/s from one process; DB one connection, about 10 light reads, 20s, 6-7 backends.
RULE SLIP TO DISCLOSE: the agent's first FASB download sent a User-Agent containing the USER'S EMAIL instead of SEC_USER_AGENT (to fasb.org / xbrl.fasb.org, a third party); every later request used SEC_USER_AGENT from pipeline/.env; recorded in the CHANGELOG. The user's email must not go to outside services (instruction): remind agents to use SEC_USER_AGENT only, on SEC and FASB.
Not tested: only one FSDS quarter and one FSN month (classification by regex + a taxonomy-relation table, precision not measured beyond the 13 R-files); no coexistence check for the new interest tag; the 1,542 count is not filtered to the operating universe; CMI and BRO not reopened; Ex. 99.1 values not compared; DQC rules checked only through the taxonomy copy; the CECL-era claims in bank-loans-and-allowance.md may carry the same date error. Next: Tag Mapper (same-period coexistence check of InterestExpenseNonoperating vs InterestExpense as an interest_expense fallback with a flag for ", net" labels: the LARGEST COVERAGE GAIN found); Standardization Lead (rule on the receivables and other-income perimeters and the collaboration revenue template); Data Engineer (yearly taxonomy diff keyed to the filing's taxonomy version + a quarterly face-presence pass over FSDS for the cohort); Tag Expert next run (re-check CECL-era claims against schema year and first filed date).

## Data-consumption audit (Data Engineer, 2026-10-24): COMPLETE; read-only (one connection, 20s, only platform backends at the start; chunked core.fact scan by company_id, 20-40 ids per chunk, all 5,283 companies, no unfiltered scan); data-team/strategy/team/DATA-CONSUMPTION-AUDIT-2026-10-24.md (uncommitted); summary folded into data-team/strategy/THE-PLAN.md section 9
Measured: Storage bucket `raw` 94 GB (companyfacts 79 GB in 44,630 objects, 8,829 distinct (cik, sha256) = 16 GB distinct / 11 GB latest-per-CIK; submissions 15 GB / 6.2 GB distinct; growth 7-10 GB per week; 92% (6,409 of 6,994) of the last 9 days' companyfacts fetches byte-identical to an earlier fetch; the same ~580 CIKs refetched nightly; SEC volume ~1,800 requests per night [~580 companyfacts + ~1,200 submissions], about 3 min at 10 req/s: the SEC rate limit is not the bottleneck); DB 47 GB (core 35, analytics 12, raw 0.09); core.fact 32 GB for 76.7M rows (11 GB table + 20 GB indexes, ~417 B/row), 10.6M dead tuples (12%), never autovacuumed; two indexes never scanned (idx_fact_raw_object_id 1.4 GB, idx_fact_supersedes_fact_id 0.8 GB; all 119 never-scanned non-unique indexes 3.1 GB; FKs make deletes depend on those two); compute max_connections 90, shared_buffers 512 MB (plan tier/cost not visible: ASSUMED). Scope split of core.fact (77.0M rows, 5,226 companies, cohort_v1_1 1,000 ids): cohort all years 24.0M (31%); cohort 2015+ 17.4M (22.5%); cohort 2015+ on mapped tags 4.5M (5.9%); non-cohort 53.0M (69%); pre-2015 all companies 16.8M (21.8%); outside cohort+2015+: 77.5%; rows on tags with no concept_mapping 72%; is_authoritative=false 44% (a 3-company sample suggests mostly same-value repeats across filings, but 7% of keys had no authoritative copy = conflicts); cohort ids 1008 and 1525 have no facts. Nightly wall time (run 39993949615, about 5 h): reprocess 125 min (dedupe, derive-interim-quarters and derive-q4 each killed at the 1800 s cap; mapper stages skipped); tag-library rebuild 90 min and failed; coverage snapshot 9 min; valuations + screener snapshot 50 min; later run 40065297098 reprocess 74 min (green); reprocess covers 327 companies (10-day filer window), likely only ~33 new each night (ASSUMED). TTM: 784,191 rows, 705,336 to archive, about 0.24 GB (ASSUMED): trivial; no canonical_fact_retired table exists yet (0098 not applied).
Ranked plan: (1) hash-dedupe before upload; refetch only on a new accession (cuts 63-68 GB and ~90% of companyfacts fetches; small effort, low risk; deleting old duplicate objects needs explicit authorization); (2) reprocess by watermark + weekly sweep (70-125 min -> 10-20 min, ASSUMED); (3) autovacuum tuning, drop the two unused indexes after any archive; (4) archive pre-2015 (16.8M rows, ~7 GB), then, only after the founder confirms scope, a hot table of cohort 2015+ plus other companies' 2015+ mapped tags (keeps 29.6M rows = 38%, saves ~19.8 GB, linear in 417 B/row, ASSUMED; risky for coverage growth; payloads stay in Storage); (5) drop repeat non-authoritative copies (up to ~14 GB) only after a dry-run. Supabase cost impact small (disk ~$0.125/GB-month, ASSUMED); the raw bucket is near a 100 GB allowance (ASSUMED), so lever 1 avoids overage; the real gain is runtime and reliability. Cannot lower: the 5.2k-company universe (canonical_fact + metric_value, 9.3 GB), the three hot indexes, the latest raw payload per CIK, raw immutability for changed filings, the nightly index and submissions diff. Untested: nightly log lines for filing-document fetches (not timestamped); pre-2014 vs pre-2015 split; exact index byte savings after an archive; whether any process reads the never-scanned indexes (idx_scan never reset); linear extrapolations; plan tier and billing. ## Fix #1 built: balance-sheet identity (total liabilities) bug (lane 3, 2026-10-24): COMPLETE for build, dry-run and tests; branch work/data-engineer/identity-fix, commit a0748b9 (pushed; cut from origin/work/data-engineer/lane1-rule-flags-v2 ee77148; separate from the debt branch); rule balance.identity_equity_basis@1, registered in common/rule_flags, with a real dry-run path, default OFF; the 20 cells are NOT yet Verifier-checked (only WMT, AT&T, VLO are filing-checked)
New module mapper/balance_identity.py; concept_fallback.resolve_all_balance_sheet_identity_fallbacks has a mode switch (off = legacy exactly; dry-run = legacy + plan file; on = corrected path replaces legacy); new CLI `balance-identity-dry-run` (read-only, 20s, plan JSONL). Corrected rule: total equity incl. NCI = (1) the equity input when it came from the NCI-inclusive tag, (2) the filed NCI-inclusive total for the same instant, (3) parent equity + MinorityInterest; temporary equity = the including-NCI tag when filed (it already holds redeemable NCI), else the parent line + redeemable NCI; liabilities = Assets - total equity - temporary equity; the equity direction = Assets - Liabilities - NCI - temporary equity; the plan records which equity tag was the input and the basis. The planner replaces only rows this fallback wrote (source_fact_ids contain all the assets and other-term source facts); rows from a real Liabilities or StockholdersEquity tag are never touched; it also fills empty cells; the write path (flag on) updates or inserts, never deletes, and writes a JSONL before-image first. Base is Assets (same canonical concept as LiabilitiesAndStockholdersEquity). The SPAC redeemable-common exit is a BVPS rule, untouched (redeemable common sits in temporary equity, correctly outside liabilities).
FILING-CHECKED planned values: WMT 2026-07-31 202,237M -> 189,115M (0000104169-26-000154 R4); AT&T 2026-06-30 319,920M -> 299,920M (0000732717-26-000297 R5; the equity input is the NCI-inclusive tag, so NCI is not subtracted twice); VLO 2020-12-31 33,814M -> 32,132M (0001035002-21-000051 R2). DRY-RUN (live DB, one connection, 20s, 8 backends, chunks of 100): scope 205 companies (all in ident_rows.tsv, not the 185 in the catalog); 7,703 cells planned = 7,596 liabilities replacements + 90 equity replacements + 17 liabilities fills; 5,425 dated 2015 or later; all 7,547 liabilities rows in ident_rows.tsv replaced + the 90 equity rows. Basis mix: filed NCI-inclusive total about 6,560 (includes cells with a temporary-equity line); equity input is the NCI-inclusive tag about 600; parent equity + NCI about 290; rest small. 49 more cells change than the catalog's "nonzero adjustment" count because the new rule uses the filed NCI-inclusive total even when no MinorityInterest fact exists (HPE 2025-04-30 changes 60M with a legacy adjustment of 0): inferred, not filing-checked. Plan file: scratchpad identity_plan_final.jsonl.

## Identity fix revised (lane 3, 2026-10-24): all five Verifier items fixed on work/data-engineer/identity-fix, commit 0e6efbf (pushed; rule balance.identity_equity_basis@1 still default OFF; fresh Verifier sample still needed; dry-run and unit evidence only)
(1) TEMP_INCL now TemporaryEquityCarryingAmountIncludingPortionAttributableToNoncontrollingInterests (core.concept 32640), loaded, test asserts spelling; the same misspelling in the feedback-inbox line fixed. (2) Redeemable NCI: only carrying-amount elements are read (balance-sheet measure by definition): the total element RedeemableNoncontrollingInterestEquityCarryingAmount, else the sum of its Common, Preferred and Other carrying components (MCK, ETP); also the including-NCI temporary-equity element, the parent line and the generic TemporaryEquityCarryingAmount; if the generic element sits beside a redeemable-NCI element the cell is HELD (overlap risk); FairValue and RedemptionValue elements are never read; if a redeemable NCI sits only on them (IPG) the cell is held as redeemable_nci_measurement_only; if a carrying-amount element is also filed the FairValue element is treated as a note and ignored. LIMIT: a tag-semantics rule, not face-placement proof; a carrying-amount element that is note-only would still be read: keep in the fresh sample. (3) One filing: every term from one filing; if assets and equity sources sit in different filings the cell is solved inside the assets filing from that filing's own equity (liabilities) or Liabilities fact (equity direction), else held mixed_vintage (also if the assets span two filings); adjustment facts only from that filing; IRM 2018-12-31 still held (the original filing has no equity fact in the loader). (4) Equity direction held unless MinorityInterest is filed in the same filing (nci_not_read); partnership elements PartnersCapitalAttributableToNoncontrollingInterest and ...IncludingPortionAttributableToNoncontrollingInterest not mapped: they hold both directions when no NCI total is read (BX, KKR equity cells); the liabilities direction is also held when NCI sits only on those elements. (5) Base = LiabilitiesAndStockholdersEquity of the same filing when filed, else Assets (BRKR 2019-03-31 now 1,298.7M).
HELD CELLS STAY IN THE DATABASE: a held cell keeps its stored value in this module, because blanking needs the reason store (migration 0100, written on main, NOT applied; the adapter is not wired into this base); the plan lists them as `hold` with the reason; 509 known-wrong stored values remain until the reason-store adapter is available: `on` should then blank held replacements with the recorded reason.
Dry-run (same 205 companies, live DB, one connection, 20s, 5 backends, chunks of 100; plan file identity_plan_final_v2.jsonl): 8,109 cells = 7,231 liabilities replacements + 16 equity replacements + 17 fills + 649 liabilities holds + 196 equity holds; 5,219 replace/fill cells dated 2015+, 466 hold cells 2015+; holds by reason: mixed_vintage 596, nci_on_unmapped_partnership_elements 132, redeemable_nci_measurement_only 105, nci_not_read 12; replacement size: under 0.1% 906; 0.1-1% 2,346; 1-5% 2,083; 5-10% 814; 10-25% 726; 25%+ 372. Against V1: 6,800 unchanged; 509 V1 replacements became holds; 378 changed value; 422 new; 16 no longer planned (now equal the stored value). The Verifier's wrong cells now: AMRX 2023-12-31 stored 3,453.0M -> 3,411.265M (filing 3,411.265M); MCK 2015-06-30 47,158M -> 45,556M (45,556M); ETP 2015-09-30 71,107M -> 47,072M (47,072M); IPG 2024-06-30 24,248.5M held (face 22,667.5M); IRM held; BX and KKR equity held; WMT 189,115M, AT&T 299,920M, VLO 2020 32,132M unchanged. The 390 "missed temporary equity" cells at 27 companies: 311 now replaced, 79 held. The 5 negative controls were not planned in V1; the planner logic leaving them alone is unchanged and a test covers it.
Tests: 29 in tests/unit/test_balance_identity.py; 34 mutants all killed after additions; full unit suite 1328 passed, 9 skipped; real CI mypy: no issues in 221 source files; CI format check clean; ruff check shows 9 B023 warnings for the in-loop `held` helper (CI does not run ruff check; left). ## Identity fix tag-based approach FROZEN as interim (lane 3, 2026-10-24): branch work/data-engineer/identity-fix head 2d7df7e (pushed; rule balance.identity_equity_basis@1 default OFF; dry-run only); DECISION (Dhairya): no third Verifier sample on it; the final design is the face read via scrooner-25's slot-record machinery (a balance-sheet family)
What the interim code does (mapper/balance_identity.py; identity = cross-check): (1) face subtotals confirm the identity: when a filing prints both LiabilitiesCurrent and LiabilitiesNoncurrent every reading of the equity and mezzanine terms is tried and only a reading that reproduces current + noncurrent (0.05% + rounding) is used, else held `identity_not_confirmed_by_face_subtotals` (catches NI's 1,360M component not on the face; FE 2017-2019: all 12 quarters held, stored values unchanged; FE 2018-03-31's stored 31,420M equals the face); (2) without subtotals only whole-line elements are read (including-NCI element, parent line, redeemable-NCI total); component elements (Common, Preferred, Other), generic TemporaryEquityCarryingAmount and FairValue/RedemptionValue elements HOLD the cell (redeemable_nci_component_not_proven_on_face, temporary_equity_generic_not_proven_on_face, redeemable_nci_measurement_only); NCI-inclusive total must equal parent equity + MinorityInterest else held; parent-only reading needs a filing with no NCI element of any kind else held (nci_unconfirmed); equity direction: no NCI accepted only when the filing has no NCI element at all (AJG case, a unit test); NCI on unmapped partnership elements holds both directions; (3) value-based vintage: several filings count as one balance sheet only when ONE filing carries both values, compared by value not accession; IRM held; "solve inside the assets filing" removed (the DVN failure); (4) a filing with temporary-equity or redeemable-NCI elements but none readable for this instant is held (temporary_equity_present_not_read). Fixtures (real data, live dry-run): WMT 202,237 -> 189,115, AT&T 319,920 -> 299,920, VLO 33,814 -> 32,132, BRKR -> 1,298.7, AMRX -> 3,411.265 (confirmed by face subtotals); held: NI, FE 12 quarters, DVN, OKE, IRM (mixed vintage), IPG, AWK (measurement-only), MCK, ETP (component not proven on face), BX/KKR equity, OXY 2018-12-31 (temporary_equity_present_not_read; stored value is right, so nothing changes); AJG outside the plan's 205 companies (real cell not exercised). NOT FIXED: TRGP 2021-09-30 still replaced with the wrong value 17,002.4M -> 10,572.8M (face 9,823.1M): no stored temporary-equity element at all (the 749.7M Series A preferred is filed only with a class-of-stock member) and no face subtotals; every cell in the same situation is at risk (a filing without face subtotals whose mezzanine line is not stored, among the 4,808 value cells dated 2015+; of the 6,710 value cells 1,935 are confirmed by face subtotals, the rest rely on the strict element rules); AWK and IPG remain false holds because "face elements only" cannot be told apart from tags; the face-statement-role check (FilingSummary / presentation) is NOT implemented. Tests: full unit suite 1337 passed, 9 skipped; real CI mypy no issues in 221 files; CI format check clean; 38 mutants all killed; the TRGP, FE and NI unit tests use synthetic values, not the real cells. Dry-run (live DB, one connection, 20s, 11 backends, chunks of 50; 205 companies + 8 fixture companies; plan file identity_plan_final_v4.jsonl): 9,410 cells = 6,676 liabilities replacements + 16 fills + 18 equity replacements + 2,506 liabilities holds + 194 equity holds; holds by reason: nci_unconfirmed 1,273; identity_not_confirmed_by_face_subtotals 507; temporary_equity_present_not_read 215; mixed_vintage 140; nci_on_unmapped_partnership_elements 139; redeemable_nci_component_not_proven_on_face 137; nci_inclusive_total_without_minority_interest 116; redeemable_nci_measurement_only 107; temporary_equity_generic_not_proven_on_face 53; nci_not_read 9; nci_inclusive_total_disagrees_with_minority_interest 4; most nci_unconfirmed holds are cells where the stored value already equals the corrected one (not planned before; DB unchanged); value cells 6,710 (4,808 dated 2015+); replacement size: under 0.1% 829; 0.1-1% 2,199; 1-5% 1,885; 5-10% 750; 10-25% 672; 25%+ 359.
NEXT: lane 3 builds the shared HOLD WRITER (work/data-engineer/hold-writer): holds -> cell_blank_reason rows (0100 contract) + archive-then-replace blanking with restore_run, idempotent, scoped by company+rule+run, dry-run per rule id; scrooner-25 takes the balance-sheet family (total liabilities from the face 'Total liabilities' or the sum of the face liability lines, equity/NCI/mezzanine slots incl. class-of-stock-member lines) after the Verifier's debt V2 verdict, with 20 fixture companies from the two identity samples.

## Hold writer built (lane 3, 2026-10-24): branch work/data-engineer/hold-writer, commit 9894cec (off origin/main; pushed); module common/hold_writer.py; rule holds.blank_writer@1 (registered in common/rule_flags, dry-run path, default OFF); nothing applied to the shared DB
Modes: off = nothing; dry_run = summaries only; on = writes (`apply_holds` raises PermissionError unless the rule is ON). Unit of work = Hold (company, data point, instant, producing rule id, reason_code, the stored value the planner saw, source fact ids, evidence, optional reference value); only instants (grain I); bad grain / unknown reason code / malformed rule id raises. (a) Reason rows via cell_store into analytics.cell_blank_reason: state defect, class guard_hidden, owner, root-cause class, rule-specific reason_code, input_refs = evidence, source_fact_ids; REASON_MAP maps 23 reason codes (identity planner's reasons + the 10 debt guard reasons via debt_reason_code()) to (state, class, owner, root cause); a unit test validates every entry against the frozen contract. (b) Blanking: mapper/canonical_archive.archive_and_remove copies the row to analytics.canonical_fact_retired (action `removed`) then removes it (never a hard delete); a before-image JSONL line is written before removal; rollback_run(conn, run_id) restores archived rows and discards that run's reasons; a second rollback does nothing; canonical_archive.py and its real-SQL test are COPIED from origin/work/period-engineer/split-rebase (not on main yet): merge via the split-rebase branch first. (c) Idempotent: a cell is blanked only if its stored value still equals the planned value (else skipped_changed); already blank with an earlier reason = skipped_done; blank without a reason only gets the reason; re-running changes nothing. Scope: one manifest (rule_run) per call; reasons carry the producing rule id; company by company, commit per company; delete scope inside cell_store.replace_rule_reasons = run + company + rule. (d) Dry-run summarize(conn, holds): read-only; per producing rule holds, would_blank, already_blank, changed_since_plan; counts by reason; magnitude buckets (<1M up to >=100B) and, when the plan carries a reference value, the ratio to it. Adapters: holds_from_plan (identity planner rows), holds_from_debt_plan (debt dry-run rows with no new value); sink(conn, holds, ...) optional hook; wiring into the planners (branches identity-fix and lane3-total-debt, neither on main) is a merge-time step. Tests: 26 in tests/unit/test_hold_writer.py on REAL SQL in a throwaway Postgres 14 (port 54329, stopped; 0100 and the 0098 archive table applied verbatim into scratch schemas; shared DB never touched); with the cell_store SQL, canonical_archive SQL and rule-flag tests 62 passed against that database; 22 mutants all killed (three first missed); default full unit suite 1386 passed, 36 skipped; real CI mypy no issues in 224 files; CI format check clean. Open: grains FY/Q/TTM not supported; restore_run replays archive rows by run id only.
COUNTS (identity V4 plan identity_plan_final_v4.jsonl, 9,410 cells; live DB, read-only, one connection, 20s, 5 backends, 48 s): 2,700 holds = 2,506 liabilities + 194 equity (1,698 dated 2015+); 2,699 would be blanked, 1 already blank, 0 changed since the plan; by reason: nci_unconfirmed 1,272; identity_not_confirmed_by_face_subtotals 507; temporary_equity_present_not_read 215; mixed_vintage 140; nci_on_unmapped_partnership_elements 139; redeemable_nci_component_not_proven_on_face 137; nci_inclusive_total_without_minority_interest 116; redeemable_nci_measurement_only 107; temporary_equity_generic_not_proven_on_face 53; nci_not_read 9; nci_inclusive_total_disagrees_with_minority_interest 4; size of the values to be blanked: 1M-100M 39; 100M-1B 184; 1B-10B 890; 10B-100B 1,553; 100B+ 33. No debt guard plan run here, so no debt count.
POLICY DECISION (Dhairya, reversible): do NOT blank a hold merely because the planner could not prove it (the 1,272 nci_unconfirmed cells are parent-equity-only readings whose stored legacy A - E value is often right; FE's stored 31,420M equals the face). Blank only with POSITIVE EVIDENCE of wrongness: the hold carries a reference_value from an independent reading and |stored - reference| / reference > 0.5%, or the reason is on an explicit known-wrong list; otherwise no blanking and no user-visible reason; record an internal evidence row (analytics.cell_evidence kind unverified_hold) and count it as kept_unverified. blank_policy per reason code in REASON_MAP (blank_if_reference_differs | blank_always_known_wrong | keep_unverified); re-run the dry-run on identity V4; FE 2018-03-31 must be KEPT; NI 2023-12-31 BLANKED if its reference differs by > 0.5%. Sent to lane 3.

## Hold-writer blank policy built (lane 3, 2026-10-24): COMPLETE; branches work/data-engineer/hold-writer a277d8d (off origin/main) and work/data-engineer/identity-fix 80b228e (planner change); nothing applied; no `on:` anywhere
MERGE ORDER: mapper/canonical_archive.py and its real-SQL test are copies from origin/work/period-engineer/split-rebase: merge split-rebase FIRST or hold-writer carries a duplicate. POLICY (common/hold_writer.py; REASON_MAP carries blank_policy per reason code; default keep): blank_if_reference_differs (11 identity codes): blank only when the hold carries an independent reference_value and |stored - reference| / |reference| > 0.5% (exactly 0.5% kept; missing or zero reference kept); blank_always_known_wrong (4 debt codes, explicit list): debt_zero_with_nonzero_tag, debt_below_instrument_tag, debt_below_lease_inclusive_total, debt_partial_component (the reason itself contradicts the stored value); keep_unverified: everything else (mixed_vintage, no_source_filing and the other debt overlap/unexplained/separability codes; never blanks even with a reference). A kept hold gets NO user-visible reason and no blanking: it writes one append-only internal analytics.cell_evidence row (producing rule id, detector_version = reason code, locator unverified_hold:<reason>, source_hash = hash of the evidence [stored value, reference, evidence refs, source fact ids], level DATA-INFERRED, recorded by hold_writer). The table's evidence_kind CHECK has no 'unverified_hold' value: adding one needs a migration (not written); `detector_audit` + the locator used instead (DECISION: accept for now). Evidence rows written once per hold via an import key; rollback_run restores blanked values but cannot delete evidence rows (append-only; they stay true). Dry-run/apply count kept_unverified (+ evidence_written). Identity planner: hold rows now carry `reference` from reference_reading() (the identity read without the terms the planner could not prove: NCI-inclusive total used only when it agrees with parent equity + MinorityInterest; mezzanine subtracted only from whole-line elements; the equity direction has no reference). Checked (planner output + filing figures from the Verifier reports, filings not opened by lane 3): FE 2018-03-31 stored 31,420M = reference 31,420M: KEPT; NI 2023-12-31 stored 24,674.3M vs reference 20,940.9M (the face figure): BLANKED (17.8%); TRGP is not a hold. Tests: real SQL on a throwaway Postgres 14 (port 54329, stopped; 0100 and 0098 archive table applied into scratch schemas; shared DB untouched): FE kept (value, one evidence row, no reason row), NI blanked (archived, removed, reason row), evidence idempotence, a keep_unverified reason with a far reference still kept, a known-wrong debt reason blanks without a reference, the 0.5% boundary, missing/zero reference, adapter carries the reference, earlier cases still pass; 33 hold-writer tests, 69 with the cell_store/canonical_archive/rule-flag SQL tests; mutants: hold-writer 31 all killed; identity-fix 22 (2 patterns no longer match; behaviours covered by tests; `ref held carries` killed by hand); full unit suite on hold-writer 1388 passed, 41 skipped; real CI mypy no issues in 224 files; format clean; identity-fix: reference tests pass, mypy and format clean, full unit suite NOT re-run after this change.
COUNTS (identity V4 plan regenerated with references: 2,700 holds, 2,366 carry a reference; live DB read-only): would_blank 462, kept_unverified 2,237, already_blank 1, changed_since_plan 0. By reason (holds / would blank / kept): nci_unconfirmed 1,273 / 58 / 1,214; identity_not_confirmed_by_face_subtotals 507 / 125 / 382; temporary_equity_present_not_read 215 / 68 / 147; mixed_vintage 140 / 0 / 140; nci_on_unmapped_partnership_elements 139 / 0 / 139; redeemable_nci_component_not_proven_on_face 137 / 108 / 29; nci_inclusive_total_without_minority_interest 116 / 17 / 99; redeemable_nci_measurement_only 107 / 61 / 46; temporary_equity_generic_not_proven_on_face 53 / 24 / 29; nci_not_read 9 / 0 / 9; nci_inclusive_total_disagrees_with_minority_interest 4 / 1 / 3. The one already-blank cell is under nci_unconfirmed. CAUTION (lane 3): many of the 462 would-blank cells sit only 0.5-5% from their reference reading; the reference is the planner's own best reading, not a filing; e.g. MCK's stored value was within 0.4% of its reference and the policy would KEEP it although the stored value was actually wrong: the policy is conservative one way and not proof the other way: a blank means "contradicted by the better reading", not "checked against a filing". The Verifier sample must include cells from the 0.5-1% and 1-5% buckets before any `on:`. DHAIRYA: the hold writer is a general tool; the identity planner is an interim superseded by the face-based balance-sheet family (scrooner-25); so verify the WRITER MECHANISM once (apply/rollback/idempotence on a copy) and sample the identity blanks only if the interim is ever enabled.

## Split re-base: 1,000-company dry-run DONE; SB-6/7/8 + RU-1 built behind flags, all OFF (Period Engineer, 2026-10-24): branch work/period-engineer/split-rebase d7fdefd (pushed; main untouched); nothing enabled, applied or written to shared tables; summary data-team/strategy/team/SPLIT-REBASE-COHORT-DRYRUN-2026-10-24.md and per-cell table doc/data-moat/team/backups/split_rebase_dryrun_cohort1000_20261024.csv.gz (12 MB, committed on the branch), both in /Users/vikash/wt-split-rebase; all figures DATA-INFERRED except the Verifier's earlier sample
1,000 of 1,000 companies done, 0 failures; peak 8 pooler/app backends (cap 30), 20s, 6 read-only connections, about 7-11 minutes per run. EVENTS: 237 confirmed at 174 companies (all with 2+ evidence kinds, 172 with all three); 52 review (42 companies); 32 pending; 759 rejected. SPLIT TABLE vs FILINGS: 101 companies in the table and confirmed from filings; 73 with splits confirmed only from filings (the table is MISSING them); 55 in the table but NOT confirmed (pre-IPO splits before any filing we hold, or IPO/merger exchange ratios stored as splits: CFG 165.582, JXN 104.96, WMG 47.72): TODAY's adjuster divides those companies' earlier periods by those ratios (wrong values on pages today, to be confirmed by the Verifier). COMPANY INVARIANT (all per-share cells hidden): 10 companies, 3,855 cells: KDP (reverse merger), CPRT, SNEX, AAON, HEI, HOMB, ANF, HWM, BRO, CELH (each has filed comparatives that disagree with the split boundary). CELLS BY CLASS (all periods / 2015+): unchanged 277,648 / 201,445; rounding only 2,670 / 1,539; was unadjusted (fixed) 6,476 / 1,881; was double-divided (fixed) 1,452 / 888; shares re-based 6,301 / 3,410; other changed 195 / 140; new 995 / 807; hidden: restated copies disagree 5,373 / 3,686; derived-only removed with RU-1 19,839 / 15,172; hidden: copies disagree after conversion 1,913 / 970; hidden: unconfirmed split candidate 2,289 / 977; hidden: company invariant 3,855 / 2,678; hidden: dividend basis pending 9,653 / 6,297; hidden: units scale suspect 753 / 391; hidden: zero share count filed 107 / 64; hidden: quarter equals year (label error) 33 / 26. The 20 largest changes with accessions are in the summary; five need a filing check: LDOS Q1 FY2014 DPS 4.48, AHR Q2 2021 DPS 0.8, DD 2018 DPS 13.68, BKNG's 2026 25:1 factor, PRAX's 1-for-15 review candidate. WRONG VALUES THE COHORT RUN CAUGHT, now fixed in the code: units errors in the latest filed copy (PCG shares x10^6, VICI DPS 262,500, DLTR 123.5 shares, SIRI zero shares); old splits re-disclosed in later filings that created a fake second split (WAB, CGNX, KR); a restatement that looked like a 3x split (CRM); detector-wrong values the dry-run would have shown: 0 after these fixes in the top-change review (the full per-cell table has NOT been independently checked).
Label shift (done): before hiding a cell as "restated" the pass drops any copy equal to the same tag's value for another period in its own filing (tests reproduce the Verifier's AMZN 0001193125-13-028520 and NVDA FY2018 10-K cases; the 18 Verifier cells were not re-tested one by one; their periods fall under the restated hide, now 5,373 cohort-wide). LLY dividend basis (done): AC-24 (declared vs paid) still open, so each DPS series stays on ONE basis: the Declared tag, with cells available only as CashPaid hidden as "dividend basis pending (AC-24)" (applies to LLY Q3 2018/2019); hiding the whole mixed series instead would cost 25,558 cells (17,859 in 2015+) instead of 9,653 (6,297) (count from the previous detector revision); one constant switches between the two. THE PAIR: the held RU-1 branch (91c6171) merged, behind `pershare.never_derive@1` plus a new `derive.periodic_inputs_only@1` for the input form gate; turning on only one of the pair raises an error; with the flags OFF behaviour is the legacy behaviour (copies of the base branch's original test files run with every flag off and pass). SB-6: the old writers (conflict fills, dedup resolver, retroactive split adjuster) skip the four re-based concepts; a test asserts exactly one writer per concept. SB-7: price metrics, EPS growth, share counts in expanded metrics and the dividend streak read the re-based values. SB-8: `scrooner-map split-rebase --ciks` (no query when OFF; prints a run id) and `split-rebase-restore --run-id`; the nightly stage runs right after `resolve-facts`. CHECKS: full unit suite 1,537 passed (SQL tests on a throwaway local Postgres); mutants 48/48 (share_basis) and 18/18 (pair guard, SB-6/7/8, gates) killed; ruff correctness and format clean; CI mypy 223 files 0 issues; migrations 0001-0099 contiguous; secrets check. Not tested: the per-cell table (Verifier sample needed); live write and restore against 0098/0099 (only on local Postgres; the migrations are not applied); the ten invariant companies (need filing checks); no unit test for expanded_metrics' switch to re-based share counts.
DECISIONS: restatement policy (hide or leave periods whose filed copies disagree with no split between them): DHAIRYA default = HIDE (5,373 cells, 3,686 in 2015+), pending the Standardization Lead; dividend series policy: DHAIRYA default = Declared with off-basis cells hidden (9,653 cells; 6,297 in 2015+) pending AC-24. NEXT: Verifier 1 samples the cohort table by class (dispatched); Release Manager applies 0098/0099 (needs the founder's words in scrooner-4d's session) and does the first 10-company run with both flags on through a dispatch input.

Not tested: the borrowings-only variant (dropped from verdicts); mid-era filings beyond those named (the scan reads one filing per record and matches labels by regex, so an unusual debt label can still slip through: the TSLA solar bonds did); why production KO is about 0.3B low from 2023 and the cause of JNJ's gap; the 28 cells left in the fresh pool. DHAIRYA RULING (delegation, Standardization Lead to confirm): securities lending payable is EXCLUDED from debt (a payable for collateral received, not a borrowing; the debt card does not count it). NEXT (sent to scrooner-25): members only from the balance-sheet statement role; exclude securities lending payable; reclass AMZN's lease line; widen the net for DueToRelatedParties*; GE's two "assumed" lines both excluded; a member with a blank face cell must not fail the hard stop when the printed face total is computable; re-run; Verifier re-checks MSFT 2014-12-31, GOOG 2015-09-30, AVGO 2019-11-03, TSLA 2017-12-31, GE 2018-12-31 and ABBV 2015-06-30 as fixtures.

## Sanity regression since 09-27 (Data Triage, 2026-10-24): PARTIAL (per-company mover list cannot be built from stored data); branch work/data-triage/sanity-regression commit 50775a7 (pushed); file /Users/vikash/wt-sanity/data-team/strategy/team/SANITY-REGRESSION-0927-2026-10-24.md (about 650 words of prose); DB read-only (about 20 queries, one connection, 20s, 13 backends vs the team cap 30); SEC 10 filings (FilingSummary + balance-sheet R pages, about 20 requests at < 3 req/s under SEC_USER_AGENT); downloads deleted; baseline NOT reset
No history exists: analytics.data_sanity_check holds one overwritten row per (company, metric) dated 10-02 or later; data_sanity_gate_baseline stores counts only; created_at/resolved_at are rewritten by the nightly reprocess, so they cannot date anything; TTM metric rows keep daily snapshots, so a proxy was used for ebitda_margin and peg_ratio (ours on 09-27 vs ours now, both against today's yfinance value); trailing_pe keeps one row per company (no proxy). HEADLINE, quick_ratio: missing_ours fell from 2,072 to 680 after commit f67a927 (10-09), which stopped nulling the metric when inventory is absent; rows evaluated rose from 2,462 to 3,851; majors rose by 238 while 1,389 more companies were compared; the major rate among evaluated rows FELL (24.5% -> 21.8%); the gate divides by all rows, so more coverage reads as a regression; same pattern: ebitda_margin evaluated +230, peg +124, P/E +199. REAL WRONG VALUES OF OURS (FILING-CHECKED): f67a927 treats MISSING inventory as ZERO, so quick ratio equals current ratio for inventory holders: of the 145 cohort majors 74 are this class; 431 of the 841 population majors equal current_ratio; the five checked against filings were all wrong: NKE 0000320187-26-000193 (inventories 7,846; stored 2.075, correct 1.387); BA 0001628280-26-050038 (88,388; stored 1.138, correct 0.331); GAP 0001628280-26-059345 (2,297; stored 1.822, correct 1.109); AZO 0001104659-26-073525 (7,559,056; stored 0.890, correct 0.137); XOM 0000034088-26-000093 (inventories 25,563 in two sub-lines with no total; stored 1.135, correct 0.847); 25 of the 74 cohort cells also have an unmapped inventory-like us-gaap tag filed in 2026 (DATA-INFERRED); also real: NFLX diluted EPS for Q4 2025 is stored as -17.14 (post-split full year minus pre-split quarters), so its P/E is negative. YFINANCE DEFINITION, not our error: Yahoo's quick ratio = (cash + short-term investments + receivables) / current liabilities; it reproduces for NKE (1.193), GAP (0.771), TGT (0.255); our formula (current assets minus inventory) / current liabilities reproduces from stored inputs in 59 of 64 cohort cells; 66 cohort majors are of this kind; ADP 0000008670-26-000030 and ICE 0001571949-26-000011 hold client funds or margin deposits in current assets (ours about 1.0 vs 0.155 and 0.024); TGT 0000027419-26-000042: ours 0.363 reproduces; T 0000732717-26-000372: the stored value is now correct and the sanity row is old. SANITY-CHECK ARTEFACT (ebitda_margin, peg): yfinance_check.py reads `value is not null` while the product (resolve.py, getLatestMetrics) takes the newest snapshot including NULL; after the 150-day TTM guard (5eeb7a0, 10-09) the check compares yfinance with an older snapshot the product hides: 296 of 850 ebitda_margin majors (64 of 155 cohort) and 28 of 511 peg majors; with the product rule ebitda_margin would have about 554 majors against a baseline of 787, and peg 483 against 445. shares_outstanding: evaluated rows flat (4,534 -> 4,549) so the +45 is not coverage; of 59 cohort majors 41 have a share-count fact more than 400 days behind the latest filing (NKE 2015, CHTR 2016) and 9 are stored as 0; APH and MNST show shares exactly HALF of Yahoo's (a split-basis problem that also halves market cap and P/E); the +45 could not be attributed. trailing_pe: nothing attributable without history; many ours-vs-Yahoo gaps are about 2x in implied EPS (HAL, OXY, DLR, ESE): unresolved. peg ranked causes: ours changed more than 2% for 353 majors (candidate commits ace07fd and ef6392b, not isolated), newly computed 58, NULL-snapshot artefact 28, unchanged 72. RECOMMENDATION: FIX; do not reset the baseline now: (1) make the sanity check follow the product rule (newest snapshot; NULL = missing_ours); (2) treat inventory as zero in quick_ratio only where company_filing_line_evidence says the line is not presented, and map the unmapped inventory tags; (3) add a rate among evaluated rows to the gate, and compare quick_ratio against Yahoo's own formula built from our inputs; (4) reset the baseline only after 1 and 2 land and a run passes; (5) hand shares staleness/zeros, the APH/MNST split basis and the P/E EPS gap to the Period Engineer. Untested: any company's actual 09-27 status; a cause for the shares +45; peg, EBITDA, P/E and shares rest on DB evidence only (all 10 filings were spent on quick_ratio). DISPATCHED: lane 3 (branch work/data-engineer/quick-ratio-fix: quick_ratio inventory rule + the sanity query + evaluated-rate column; flag OFF; baseline and threshold unchanged); Period Engineer queue (shares staleness/zeros, APH/MNST, P/E EPS gap, NFLX Q4 2025). LIVE BUG: f67a927 is on main since 10-09: 74 cohort cells (431 population majors) show quick ratio = current ratio.

## Verifier check of the 1,000-company split table (2026-10-24): GATE FAILS: 14 shown values wrong (13 FILING-CHECKED, 1 [GLPI 2020-04-29] DATA-INFERRED) + 6 unresolved; ALL IN ONE CLASS (`other_changed`); the split arithmetic is clean; data-team/strategy/team/VERIFY-SPLIT-REBASE-1000-2026-10-24.md; branch origin/work/period-engineer/split-rebase d7fdefd; seed 20261024-split1000; table 340,719 rows; the rationale not read first
Clean: all 190 sampled shown cells in unadjusted, double-divided, shares, new, unchanged and 20-largest classes equal the chosen filing's value divided by the factor (multiplied for share counts); 31 cells changed by more than the factor all checked against the period's own filing and right (AWI, DRI, ODFL, NOW, MLI, NVDA, GE x8, CHDN, VRNS, DUK, AOS ...). FAILURES (all `other_changed`, 195 rows checked against the first three 10-Q/10-K filings per period): in 13 of 14 today's stored value is RIGHT and the new code makes it wrong: OHI Q1 2019 DPS -0.66 (right 0.66); BWA FY2019 DPS 0 (0.68, 140M paid); TEX Q2 2020 DPS 0.12 (0); TRMB Q4 2016 diluted EPS -0.14 (0.15); DCI Q4 FY2015 EPS 0.44 (0.41); XYL Q3 2019 DPS 0.26 (0.24), Q3 2023 0.36 (0.33); OGE Q1 2020 DPS 1.505 (0.3875); GLPI 2018-04-24 0.60 (0.63), 2021-05-20 0.705 (0.67), 2020-04-29 0.67 (0.60, DATA-INFERRED); TSCO 2023-02-08 0.22 (0.206; today's 1.03 also wrong, unadjusted); WTRG FY2024 0.3255 (1.2652) and FY2023 0.3071 (1.1882). UNRESOLVED (showing not supported): COO Q1 FY2020 DPS 0 (copies disagree 0 vs 0.03); LDOS May-Jul 2012 EPS 0.72 (original 0.32 x4 = 1.28); IONS FY2019 basic 2.00 / diluted 1.90 vs 2.12 / 2.08 originally; HSIC Q2 2019 basic 0.77 vs 0.79. CAUSE: the new code takes one copy of each value, often from a LATER filing; some later filings shift comparative quarters by a year (TRMB, DCI) or put the current dividend rate on a prior period (XYL, TSCO, GLPI); others are one-off filer errors (OHI, BWA, WTRG, OGE, TEX); the restated-copies guard did not fire even where copies disagree (OGE, XYL, GLPI, TSCO, COO). HOLDS: false holds (correct values hidden): TOL Q2 FY21 0.17; LEA 9M 2014 0.60; SAIA and EXEL share counts; C Q1 2012 and CF 2007 DPS; CPRT, AAON, HEI invariant holds (HOMB, ANF, CELH, SNEX likely false too); the quarter-equals-year holds (BPOP Q4 2016 0.15 and similar); 20 PRAX share cells (the 1-for-15 split is real, SEC fact 0.0667; EOG likely); dividend-basis holds are correct values hidden by policy. Justified holds: RU-1 (23 of 25 have no filed 3-month fact; the other 2 are CP values filed in CAD); zero share counts, BRO, ENSG, PCG, RAL, SFD, AHR and BF-A conversion conflicts. NAMED ITEMS right: LDOS 4.48 as filed; AHR 0.8; DD 13.68 (2026 1-for-3 reverse split FILING-CHECKED, shares 409.9M -> 135.0M, 0001666700-26-000053); BKNG 25:1; PRAX's one shown cell; the code-fixed cases (PCG, VICI, DLTR, SIRI, WAB, CGNX, KR, CRM). WRONG TODAY, fixed by the new pass: CB and LOGI DPS stored in CHF; CFG, JXN, WMG, SFD, PACS, VG, RVMD, AXSM, KNSA divided by IPO/merger ratios (CFG 2012 EPS 0.00695 vs filed 1.15); unadjusted NOW, DRI, PINS, PRAX; double-divided DD, AOS, CHDN, LDOS; CP EPS in CAD shown as USD; 107 zero share counts. The new pass leaves all 67 split-table companies it does not confirm at factor 1 (67, not 55: AWI, AAON, CPRT, SNEX included). Not tested: plausibility of the large fixed classes beyond the 90 sampled cells; hidden rows with no candidate values (judged on split arithmetic only); ULTA, HWM, KDP; the code itself. DHAIRYA DECISION: PHASE 1 SWITCH: exclude the `other_changed` class from writes (stored value kept, no reason) so the clean classes ship first; fixes (sent to the Period Engineer): original-filing agreement before showing; same-filing dividend checks; release the false holds; re-run the dry-run and re-send other_changed for a full recheck.

## Verifier V4 check of the 30-company debt slot set (2026-10-24): FAILS the gate: ONE slot value wrong + the new face-admission rule is unsound as written; data-team/strategy/team/VERIFY-SLOT-RECORDS-30SET-V4-2026-10-24.md (uncommitted); branch work/scrooner-25/slot-records @ 9696f27 (173 records, not 174); about 420 SEC requests at <= 1.7/s (SEC_USER_AGENT), no DB, downloads deleted, free disk 8.5 GB; scripts in scratchpad vsr4/
CUSTOM-TAG PART PASSES: every record with a custom member is flagged; every replay cell touching one lands in needs_instance_reader; 24 records with custom members all flagged; 0 note-only members; the single face-admitted member (AVGO) is right. WRONG VALUE: COST 2018-09-02: slot 6,487M, right 6,577M (production right): the FY2018 10-K face shows only noncurrent long-term debt; the 90M current portion is folded into another current line (0000909832-18-000013 R2); the debt note gives the carrying total 6,577 = 90 + 6,487 (R39); the next 10-Q prints the 90M as its own line (0000909832-19-000003 R2); a record built from face lines alone cannot see this: tie each era's sum to the filer's own debt-note carrying total and send any gap to review; the earlier V2 verdict on COST 2018-11-25 still holds (current portion 0 there; slot 6,480M right). Fixtures: MSFT 2014-12-31 (28,308M), GOOG 2015-09-30 (5,231M), AVGO 2019-11-03 (32,798M) right; GE 2015-12-31, RPAY 2020-03-31, TSLA 2017-12-31 correctly in needs_instance_reader (face totals 197,602M, 246.5M, 10,314,868K), but GE and RPAY still carry a partial value (194,519M and 5.5M): null it in that state so nothing downstream can publish it; ABBV 2015-06-30 hard-stops honestly (no printed filer total to confirm the blank; the value would be right). FACE-ADMISSION RULE (face_line_not_in_statement_cal): fires once in the 33-company set (AVGO, correctly); simulated on other filings from the 1,000-company extract: admits CORRECTLY in 6 filings (AVGO, LMCA x2, Allison, Antero, NFG long-term debt, EW) and WRONGLY in 6: components under a face subtotal that is already a member (double count): CenterPoint 0001130310-26-000008 (19,902 inside 20,566), Dominion 0001193125-15-363617 (19,790 inside 23,245), Watsco 0001193125-25-041730 (revolver and finance leases inside "Total long-term obligations", which also includes 321,715 of operating leases); a total admitted beside its own parts: ICE 0001571949-17-000003 (6,364 = 2,493 + 3,871), Textron 0000217346-21-000019 (3,707 = 509 + 3,198); an asset with a debt-like name: NFG 0000070145-19-000013 "Unamortized Debt Expense" 14,922; header-only "Abstract" rows also match (would turn computable cells into hard stops). GUARDS NEEDED: admit only rows inside the liabilities section; never admit a row under a member total or one equal to the sum of members; skip Abstract tags; tie the result to the printed liability totals. FRESH DRAW (random.Random(20261027), no cell read before; fresh pools fragment 35, precision 35, composition 42, production-higher 0): slot right 9/10 (COST 2018-09-02 the one wrong); production wrong 9/10 (LRCX its borrowings-only perimeter); the 5 custom-tag cells (KO 2016-12 and 2017-06, CAT 2016-12 and 2019-06, TSLA 2015-06) have no replay rows: checked at record level: each face line is a custom tag and the record flags it needs_instance_reader: all 5 right. Re-scan of all records: 0 note-only members; 24 records with custom members (all flagged); 0 members missing the custom field. Production errors found this pass: CVX 2015/2021, CSCO 2019/2026, MRK rounding, INTC +65M, JNJ +1,749M, UNH -625M, COST 2018-11-25 (face value used instead of carrying value). Durable notes: 2 ledger rows, 2 tag-inbox lines (UnamortizedLossReacquiredDebtNoncurrent, OtherLongTermDebtNoncurrent), mind entry (face-line admission for debt needs guards). Not repeated: the full scan for missing face lines (V2 found none). NEXT (sent to scrooner-25): the four guards with 6 must-not-admit and 6 must-admit fixtures; the debt-note tie-out per cell (COST); NULL the value in needs_instance_reader; re-run; the Verifier re-checks COST and the 12 rule fixtures; then plan + effort for the INSTANCE READER (the 24-record worklist).

Anchors at the CORRECT instants (bs_anchors.json had wrong dates: EMR 2024-12-31 not 2016-09-30; WMT 2026-07-31 not 2015-04-30; BX 2019-06-30 not 2021-12-31): right: WMT 189,115M, VLO 32,132M, EMR 16,231M, DIS 102,321M, FE 31,420M, DVN 15,212M, BX parent 6,308.355M, KKR parent 5,947.415M; BRKR and TRGP holds right; WRONG: T 2026-06-30 165,289M vs 299,920M (long-term debt dropped), AWK 2017-12-31 14,089M vs 14,097M, MCK 2015-06-30 45,552M vs 45,556M, NI 2023-12-31 21,013.4M vs 20,940.9M (held; wrong if released). CORRECTION: the "AJG 2019-01-01 equity 3,000,568K" cell is BROWN & BROWN (CIK 0000079282, BRO), mislabelled AJG in the V2 identity report (corrected there and in the mind entry); the fixture AJG is CIK 0000354190. SIX DEFECT CLASSES: (1) a subtotal swallows the standalone line before it (T, OXY, all 46 instants): bs_totals_detect.py: after "Total current liabilities" the next line (Long-term debt) starts a new run with no section header above it; the later "Total deferred credits and other (noncurrent) liabilities" runs skeleton[run_start:] = [t] and replaces the long-term debt line with a subtotal that does not contain it; missing long-term debt: T 2026-06-30 134,631M (0000732717-26-000297 R5), T 2016-09-30 117,239M (0000732717-16-000233 R5), OXY 2018-12-31 10,201M (0000797468-19-000004 R2), OXY 2015-09-30 6,882M (0000797468-15-000014 R2), OXY 2025-09-30 20,846M (0001628280-25-051071 R2); fix: members from the filer's calc children or presentation tree. (2) asset/equity rows in the liabilities list (MCK equity line "Other (4)" OtherAdditionalCapital; NI 2025-09-30 PrepaidExpenseCurrent 157.4M [23,318.5M vs 23,161.1M]; ET 2025-06-30 cash 242M [79,407M vs 79,165M]); record scan: MCK 13 eras, NI 2024-2026, ET 2025 (DATA-INFERRED). (3) rows rendered after the L&E total counted again (NI: "Asset Retirement Obligation, Current 72.5" already inside Total Current Liabilities; NI 2025-03-31 prepaid 138.2M after the total: 22,297.3M vs 22,159.1M). (4) redeemable preferred as mezzanine when the face puts it inside debt (AWK; 2016 era 12,542M vs 12,553M, preferred 11M). (5) members and cross-check inputs across filings (DVN 2018-03-31 15,577M vs 15,591M [0001564590-18-010177 R4; the ARO member came from the Q1 2019 10-Q and the 14M gap sat inside the 0.05% tolerance 14.7M]; DVN 2017-09-30 15,611M vs 15,625M; BRKR 2015-12-31 ties at 997.1M on the restated FY2016 copy while the FY2015 10-K prints 998.0M; OXY, FE): `distinct on (tag, end_date)` picks the latest copy across filings. (6) the partnership NCI slot and OXY's equity slots not filled (BX NCI 7,655.4M; KKR 46,050.7M; OXY equity_parent/NCI None in 4 eras; OXY 2023-03-31 lists a current-debt component next to its own current-liabilities subtotal; not filing-checked). RANDOM SAMPLE (seed random.Random(20261027); frame 459 ties + 191 gaps since 2015, sorted by (ticker, end), shuffled, at most 2 per company): 20 ties: 18 right, 1 wrong (DVN 2018-03-31), 1 not adjudicated (GE 2015, an old multi-entity R page); 15 gaps: 6 real composition misses (T x2, OXY x2, AWK x2), 2 wrong from other classes (NI 2025-09-30, DVN 2017-09-30), 7 where liabilities are right and the gap comes from the cross-check's own inputs (GE x2, FE x2, KKR x2, BX); none is filer rounding. ERA BOUNDARIES (6 filings): right NI capitalization 2015 17,429.2M, FE capitalization 2017 37,073M, ETP predecessor 2015 44,165M (mezzanine 33M + 15M on the face); wrong AWK 2016, NI 2025-03-31, ET 2025-06-30. IS THE IDENTITY ONLY A CROSS-CHECK? Yes, and weak: MCK (4M), AWK (8M), DVN (14M) pass inside 0.05% and are wrong; BRKR 2015-12-31 exact tie wrong for that filing; the replay's own inputs mix vintages; PROPOSAL: tolerance = the filer's rounding unit; every term from ONE accession. Not tested: GE's multi-entity layout; the other fixture companies' era boundaries; mezzanine values that exist only with a dimension member; a second reader. NEXT (sent to scrooner-25): fix the six classes, re-run with same-accession inputs and a rounding-unit tolerance; build subtotals from the filer's calc/presentation tree and classify each row by balance type as a guard; the Verifier checks a fresh sample including every MCK, NI, ET and AWK era.
